the forces of nature = 自然的力量
③ Mother Nature gave the islands warm seas and almost no fresh water.
= 大自然给了那些岛屿温暖的海水，却几乎没给淡水。
Mother Nature = 大自然（拟人说法）
④ She is cautious by nature and reads every contract twice.
= 她生性谨慎，每份合同都读两遍。
by nature = 生性
⑤ It is not in his nature to bear a grudge for long.
= 他不是那种会长久记仇的人。
it is not in somebody's nature to do something = 某人不是那种会做某事的人
⑥ It is only human nature to want what the neighbours have.
= 想要邻居有的东西，不过是人性使然。
human nature = 人性
⑦ The dog has a gentle nature and has never snapped at a child.
= 那条狗性情温顺，从没冲孩子龇过牙。
a gentle nature = 温顺的性情
⑧ The doctors have not yet established the exact nature of the illness.
= 医生们还没确定那种病的确切性质。
the nature of something = 某事的性质
⑨ Decisions of this nature should be taken by the full committee.
= 这一类决定应当由全体委员作出。
of this nature = 这一类的
⑩ His remarks were in the nature of a warning rather than a threat.
= 他的话算是一种警告，而不是威胁。
in the nature of = 属于…一类
⑪ In the nature of things, young people want to leave a village like this one.
= 按常理，年轻人总想离开这样的村子。
in the nature of things = 按事物的常理
⑫ After twenty years, locking the workshop door had become second nature to him.
= 二十年下来，锁作坊的门对他来说已经成了习惯。
second nature = 习惯成自然的事
become second nature to somebody = 对某人成了习惯
⑬ They sold the house in town and went back to nature on a hill farm.
= 他们卖了城里的房子，到一座山地农场回归自然去了。
back to nature = 回归自然
⑭ He left the meeting for a moment to answer the call of nature.
= 他离开会场片刻，去方便一下。
the call of nature = 要上厕所（委婉）
⑮ The vet said there was nothing more to do but let nature take its course.
= 兽医说，没别的可做了，只能顺其自然。
let nature take its course = 顺其自然
⑯ The marsh was made a nature reserve to protect the birds that winter there.
= 那片沼泽划成了自然保护区，为的是保护在那儿越冬的鸟。
a nature reserve = 自然保护区
⑰ The children followed a nature trail through the wood with a list of leaves to find.
= 孩子们拿着一张要找的树叶清单，顺着一条自然观察小径穿过树林。
a nature trail = 自然观察小径

naturism
/ˈneɪtʃərɪzəm/
核心：裸体主义：认为在户外不穿衣服有益身心的主张与做法，英式多用这个词，美式多说 nudism。不可数。
奉行的人叫 naturist，也作定语：a naturist beach。并入本条。
① Naturism is permitted on two beaches along that stretch of coast.
= 那一段海岸有两处海滩允许裸体活动。
naturism = 裸体主义
② The far end of the bay is a naturist beach, marked by a small sign.
= 海湾的尽头是一片裸体海滩，立着一块小牌子。
a naturist beach = 裸体海滩
③ A group of naturists has rented the campsite every August for thirty years.
= 一群裸体主义者三十年来每年八月都租下那片营地。
a naturist = 裸体主义者

naturopathy
/ˌneɪtʃəˈrɒpəθi/
核心：自然疗法：不用药物，靠饮食、锻炼、草药、按摩来防病治病的一套做法，属于替代医学。不可数。
从业者叫 naturopath，形容词 naturopathic。并入本条。
① She turned to naturopathy when years of tablets had failed to cure her headaches.
= 吃了多年药片都没治好头痛，她转向了自然疗法。
turn to naturopathy = 转向自然疗法
② The naturopath told him to give up coffee and sleep with the window open.
= 那位自然疗法师让他戒掉咖啡，开着窗睡觉。
a naturopath = 自然疗法师
③ The clinic offers naturopathic treatments alongside ordinary medical care.
= 那家诊所在常规医疗之外，也提供自然疗法。
naturopathic treatments = 自然疗法的治疗

naught
/nɔːt/
核心：两支，旧式、书面，是 nought 的另一种拼法。一是「无、零」，如今只活在固定说法里：
come to naught 是落空、化为乌有，be for naught 是白费；二是 set something at naught，（正式）不把某事放在眼里。
① All their plans for the summer came to naught when the car broke down.
= 汽车一坏，他们整个夏天的计划全落了空。
come to naught = 落空、化为乌有
② Three years of patient work had been for naught.
= 三年耐心的工夫全白费了。
be for naught = 白费
③ The new owner set the old agreements at naught and doubled the rent.
= 新业主不把旧协议放在眼里，把租金翻了一倍。
set something at naught = 不把某事放在眼里

naughty
/ˈnɔːti/
核心：三支。一是（孩子）淘气的、不听话的；大人对自己用是开玩笑：it was naughty of me；
二是（口语，诙谐）有点出格的、带点色情意味的；三是 naughty but nice，明知不该却忍不住的享受。
副词 naughtily 与名词 naughtiness 并入本条。
naughty step 是罚淘气孩子去坐着反省的那级楼梯，并入本条。
① The naughty boy had hidden the teacher's glasses in the piano.
= 那个淘气的男孩把老师的眼镜藏进了钢琴里。
a naughty boy = 淘气的男孩
② It was naughty of me to finish the chocolates without telling anyone.
= 我没跟人说就把巧克力吃光了，真不应该。
it was naughty of me = 我真不应该
大人自嘲的说法。
③ The comedian told a few naughty jokes once the children had gone to bed.
= 等孩子们上床睡了，那位喜剧演员讲了几个荤段子。
a naughty joke = 荤段子
④ A cream cake in the middle of the afternoon is naughty but nice.
= 下午三四点钟来一块奶油蛋糕，明知不该，却真是享受。
naughty but nice = 明知不该却忍不住的享受
⑤ The little girl grinned naughtily and dropped another pea into the water jug.
= 小女孩调皮地咧嘴一笑，又往水壶里丢了一颗豌豆。
grin naughtily = 调皮地咧嘴笑
⑥ The teacher put his naughtiness down to boredom and gave him harder sums.
= 老师把他的淘气归因于无聊，给了他更难的算术题。
somebody's naughtiness = 某人的淘气
⑦ He spent five minutes on the naughty step for throwing his dinner on the floor.
= 他把饭扔到地上，在反省台阶上坐了五分钟。
the naughty step = 反省台阶

Nauruan
/naʊˈruːən/
核心：两支。一是形容词，瑙鲁的；二是名词，瑙鲁人，可数，也指瑙鲁语。首字母大写。
① The Nauruan economy once depended almost entirely on the export of phosphate.
= 瑙鲁的经济一度几乎全靠出口磷酸盐。
the Nauruan economy = 瑙鲁经济
② A Nauruan carried the flag for the smallest team at the games.
= 那届运动会上，一名瑙鲁选手为人数最少的代表队举旗。
a Nauruan = 瑙鲁人

nausea
/ˈnɔːziə/
核心：恶心、想吐的感觉：不可数，正式或医学说法，日常说 feel sick。也引申为强烈的厌恶。
动词 nauseate 是使恶心、使厌恶；形容词 nauseating 是令人作呕的，副词 nauseatingly；
形容词 nauseous 一是觉得恶心的，二是让人恶心的。都并入本条。
① A wave of nausea swept over her as the boat left the harbour.
= 船一离开港口，一阵恶心向她袭来。
a wave of nausea = 一阵恶心
② Possible side effects include nausea, dizziness and loss of appetite.
= 可能的副作用包括恶心、头晕和食欲减退。
nausea and dizziness = 恶心头晕
③ The smell of frying fat nauseated him for weeks after the illness.
= 病后好几个星期，一闻到煎油的味道他就恶心。
nauseate somebody = 使某人恶心
④ The way he flatters the boss is nauseating to everyone else in the office.
= 他拍老板马屁的那副样子，让办公室里别的人看了作呕。
be nauseating = 令人作呕
⑤ The sequel is nauseatingly sweet from the first scene to the last.
= 那部续集从头一场到最后一场，都甜得发腻。
nauseatingly sweet = 甜得发腻
⑥ The winding road up the mountain made the children feel nauseous.
= 上山的路弯弯曲曲，孩子们觉得想吐。
feel nauseous = 觉得想吐
⑦ A nauseous smell of rotting fish hung over the quay.
= 码头上弥漫着一股烂鱼的恶心气味。
a nauseous smell = 令人恶心的气味

nautical
/ˈnɔːtɪkəl/
核心：航海的、与船和水手有关的。多作定语：nautical terms（航海用语）、a nautical chart（海图）。
也说带海洋风的装饰与衣着。nautical mile 是海里，约一千八百五十二米，并入本条。
① The novel is full of nautical terms that send most readers to the dictionary.
= 那部小说里满是航海用语，多数读者得去查词典。
nautical terms = 航海用语
② The captain spread a nautical chart on the table and marked the reef in red.
= 船长把一张海图摊在桌上，用红笔标出了那片礁石。
a nautical chart = 海图
③ The café has a nautical theme, with ropes, lanterns and a ship's wheel on the wall.
= 那家咖啡馆走的是航海风，墙上挂着缆绳、提灯和一只舵轮。
a nautical theme = 航海风
④ The lighthouse can be seen from twenty nautical miles away on a clear night.
= 晴朗的夜晚，二十海里之外就能看见那座灯塔。
a nautical mile = 海里

nautilus
/ˈnɔːtɪləs/
核心：两支，可数。一是鹦鹉螺：生活在热带海洋里的一种软体动物，外壳呈螺旋形，里面分成一个个小室；
二是它的壳，因螺线匀称常被拿来作图案与比喻。复数 nautiluses 或 nautili。
① The nautilus rises and sinks by changing the amount of gas in its shell.
= 鹦鹉螺靠改变壳里气体的多少来上浮和下沉。
a nautilus = 鹦鹉螺
② A nautilus shell cut in half shows a perfect spiral of small chambers.
= 把鹦鹉螺的壳对半切开，能看到由一个个小室排成的完美螺线。
a nautilus shell = 鹦鹉螺壳

Navajo
/ˈnævəhəʊ/
核心：三支。一是纳瓦霍人：美国西南部的一支原住民，可数，复数 Navajo 或 Navajos；二是纳瓦霍语，不可数；
三是作定语，纳瓦霍人的。首字母大写，也拼作 Navaho。
① The Navajo have lived among these canyons for many hundreds of years.
= 纳瓦霍人在这片峡谷之间已经生活了好几百年。
the Navajo = 纳瓦霍人
② Her grandmother speaks only Navajo, so the children translate at the clinic.
= 她祖母只会说纳瓦霍语，所以到了诊所由孩子们翻译。
speak Navajo = 说纳瓦霍语
③ He bought a Navajo rug woven in red, black and cream from a roadside stall.
= 他在路边摊买了一条红、黑、米三色织成的纳瓦霍地毯。
a Navajo rug = 纳瓦霍地毯

naval
/ˈneɪvəl/
核心：海军的、与军舰有关的。只作定语：a naval base（海军基地）、a naval officer（海军军官）、a naval battle（海战）。
读音同 navel，拼写只差一个字母。
① The town grew up around a naval base that employs half its people.
= 那座镇是围着一个海军基地发展起来的，镇上一半人靠基地吃饭。
a naval base = 海军基地
② Her father was a naval officer who spent months at a time at sea.
= 她父亲是海军军官，一出海就是好几个月。
a naval officer = 海军军官
③ The museum has a model of the naval battle with four hundred tiny ships.
= 博物馆里有那场海战的模型，上面有四百艘小船。
a naval battle = 海战

Navaratri
/ˌnʌvəˈrɑːtri/
核心：两支。一是九夜节：印度教的一个节日，连续九个夜晚敬奉女神，秋天举行；二是这九夜里的庆祝活动。
不可数，首字母大写，前面不加冠词，介词用 at、during。
① Navaratri falls in October this year, a fortnight before the festival of lights.
= 今年的九夜节在十月，比灯节早两个星期。
Navaratri = 九夜节
② During Navaratri the temple hall is full of dancers until long after midnight.
= 九夜节期间，庙里的大厅挤满跳舞的人，一直到后半夜。
during Navaratri = 九夜节期间

nave
/neɪv/
核心：两支，可数。一是（教堂的）中殿：从大门到圣坛之间、会众坐的那一长条主体空间；二是建筑描述里与侧廊相对的那部分。
① The bride walked slowly down the nave on her grandfather's arm.
= 新娘挽着祖父的胳膊，沿着中殿慢慢往前走。
walk down the nave = 沿中殿往前走
② The nave is Norman, but the two side aisles were added three centuries later.
= 中殿是诺曼时期的，两边的侧廊是三个世纪以后加的。
the nave = 中殿

navel
/ˈneɪvəl/
核心：肚脐，可数，口语说 belly button。读音同 naval。
navel-gazing 是（贬）只顾琢磨自己、不看外面的自我沉溺，不可数；navel orange 是脐橙。都并入本条。
① The scar runs from just below her navel to her right hip.
= 那道疤从她肚脐下面一点，一直到右胯。
below the navel = 肚脐下面
② She had her navel pierced at sixteen without telling her parents.
= 她十六岁那年瞒着父母穿了脐环。
have your navel pierced = 穿脐环
③ The party has spent a year navel-gazing instead of talking to voters.
= 那个党花了一年时间自我琢磨，却不去跟选民说话。
navel-gazing = 自我沉溺
含贬义：只盯着自己的肚脐看。
④ She peeled a navel orange and shared the segments round the table.
= 她剥了一个脐橙，把橙瓣分给桌上的人。
a navel orange = 脐橙

navigate
/ˈnævɪɡeɪt/
核心：四支。一是导航、认路：靠地图、仪器确定路线，开车时指坐在副驾上指路；二是驾船、驾机航行于，或小心地穿过难走的地方；
三是在复杂的制度、局面里找到路子；四是浏览网站、在页面之间找路。
形容词 navigable 一是（河道）可通航的，二是（网站）好找路的；名词 navigability。并入本条。
① I will drive if you navigate, because I cannot read a map and steer at once.
= 你来看路我来开，我没法一边看地图一边握方向盘。
navigate = 看图指路
② Early sailors navigated by the stars and the colour of the water.
= 早期的水手靠星星和海水的颜色导航。
navigate by the stars = 靠星星导航
③ The pilot navigated the tanker through the narrow channel at high tide.
= 领航员趁涨潮把那艘油轮带过了狭窄的航道。
navigate a ship through something = 驾船穿过某处
④ We navigated our way through the crowd to the far side of the square.
= 我们从人群里一路穿过去，到了广场的另一头。
navigate your way through something = 一路穿过某处
⑤ The leaflet helps patients to navigate the benefits system after leaving hospital.
= 那份小册子帮助病人出院以后摸清福利制度的门道。
navigate a system = 摸清一套制度的门道
⑥ The site is easy to navigate, with a clear menu at the top of every page.
= 那个网站很好找路，每一页顶上都有清楚的菜单。
easy to navigate = 好找路的
⑦ The river is navigable for sixty miles above the port.
= 那条河从港口往上六十英里都可以通航。
be navigable = 可通航的
⑧ The dam improved the navigability of the lower river during the dry season.
= 那座水坝改善了下游河段在旱季的通航条件。
the navigability of a river = 河道的通航条件

navigation
/ˌnævɪˈɡeɪʃən/
核心：三支，不可数。一是导航、航行术：确定位置与路线的本事与过程；二是（船只、飞机的）航行、通行；
三是在网站、软件里找路的方式。形容词 navigational 并入本条：navigational aids 是助航设备。
汽车上说 satellite navigation（卫星导航），口语截短成 satnav。navigation bar 是网页的导航栏，并入本条。
① Navigation was difficult in the fog, and the ferry was two hours late.
= 雾里很难导航，渡船晚了两个钟头。
navigation = 导航
② The car has satellite navigation that speaks in a choice of nine voices.
= 那辆车有卫星导航，有九种语音可选。
satellite navigation = 卫星导航
③ The canal was closed to navigation while the lock gates were repaired.
= 修闸门期间，那条运河停止通航。
closed to navigation = 停止通航
④ The lighthouse is still one of the most important navigational aids on the coast.
= 那座灯塔至今仍是这段海岸最重要的助航设施之一。
navigational aids = 助航设施
⑤ The search box sits at the right-hand end of the navigation bar.
= 搜索框在导航栏的最右端。
a navigation bar = 导航栏

navigator
/ˈnævɪɡeɪtə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是领航员、导航员：船上、飞机上负责定位与定航线的人，也指汽车拉力赛里看路书的副驾；
二是（历史）早期出海探索的航海家。
① The navigator worked out the plane's position from the stars every half hour.
= 领航员每半小时靠星星算一次飞机的位置。
a navigator = 领航员
② The driver trusted his navigator and took the blind corner at full speed.
= 车手信得过他的领航员，全速冲过了那个看不见前方的弯。
a rally navigator = 拉力赛领航员
③ The Portuguese navigators of the fifteenth century mapped the coast of Africa.
= 十五世纪的葡萄牙航海家测绘了非洲的海岸。
the Portuguese navigators = 葡萄牙航海家

navvy
/ˈnævi/
核心：两支，英式，较旧，可数。一是挖土工：从前修运河、铁路、公路时干重体力活的工人；二是泛指干粗重活的苦力。
复数 navvies。
① Thousands of navvies dug the canal by hand with picks and shovels.
= 成千上万的挖土工用镐和铁锹，一下一下挖出了那条运河。
a navvy = 挖土工
② He worked like a navvy all weekend to clear the garden before the rain.
= 他整个周末像苦力一样干活，赶在下雨前把花园清理出来。
work like a navvy = 像苦力一样干活

navy
/ˈneɪvi/
核心：两支。一是海军：一国在海上作战的军队，可数，the navy、the Navy；英式里作主语时动词单复数都行。
join the navy 是参加海军，in the navy 是在海军服役。二是藏青色、海军蓝，不可数，也作形容词，全称 navy blue。
navy bean 是美式说法，指白芸豆，做焗豆用的那种，并入本条。
① He joined the navy at seventeen and saw the world from an engine room.
= 他十七岁参加海军，在机舱里看遍了世界。
join the navy = 参加海军
② She served in the navy for twelve years as a radar engineer before retraining as a teacher.
= 她在海军当了十二年雷达工程师，后来改行当了教师。
in the navy = 在海军服役
③ The navy has sent two ships to patrol the strait.
= 海军已派出两艘军舰在海峡巡逻。
the navy = 海军
④ She wore a navy suit and a white shirt to the interview.
= 她穿了一身藏青色套装配白衬衫去面试。
a navy suit = 藏青色套装
⑤ The school uniform is a navy blue jumper and grey trousers.
= 校服是藏青色的套头衫配灰裤子。
navy blue = 藏青色
⑥ She soaked the navy beans overnight and baked them slowly with onion.
= 她把白芸豆泡了一夜，和洋葱一起慢慢焗。
a navy bean = 白芸豆
美式说法。

nay
/neɪ/
核心：三支，旧式或书面。一是副词，用来加码：不止如此、甚至，放在一个更强的说法前面；
二是（旧）等于 no；三是名词，（表决时的）反对票，跟 yea、aye 相对。
① The journey was difficult, nay impossible, without a guide.
= 没有向导，那趟路很难走，不，简直走不了。
difficult, nay impossible = 很难，甚至根本不可能
用更强的词把前一个词顶掉。
② "Nay, sir, I will not leave until the debt is paid," said the old man.
= 老人说：「不，先生，账不还清我不走。」
nay = 不（旧式）
③ The motion was lost by thirty-one yeas to forty nays.
= 那项动议以三十一票赞成、四十票反对被否决。
a nay = 一张反对票

naysayer
/ˈneɪseɪə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是唱反调的人：对新计划、新想法总说不行的人，多含贬义；二是泛指持怀疑态度、不肯附和的批评者。
① The naysayers said the bridge could never be built, and it opened on time.
= 唱反调的人说那座桥永远建不成，结果它如期通车了。
the naysayers = 唱反调的人
② She proved the naysayers wrong by finishing the race in under four hours.
= 她不到四个小时跑完全程，证明那些不看好她的人错了。
prove the naysayers wrong = 证明唱反调的人错了

Nazi
/ˈnɑːtsi/
核心：两支。一是名词，纳粹分子：一九三三至一九四五年统治德国的国家社会主义党的成员，可数；也作定语，纳粹的。
二是（贬，冒犯）拿来骂专横、不容异己的人。首字母大写。
① Her grandparents fled Vienna in 1938 when the Nazis marched in.
= 一九三八年纳粹开进维也纳的时候，她的祖父母逃离了那里。
the Nazis = 纳粹
② The museum documents life in Nazi Germany through the letters of ordinary families.
= 那座博物馆通过普通人家的书信，记录纳粹德国时期的生活。
Nazi Germany = 纳粹德国
③ He was rightly criticised for calling the parking wardens Nazis.
= 他把停车管理员骂作纳粹，受到批评是应该的。
call somebody a Nazi = 骂某人是纳粹
这一支是冒犯的骂人话，拿那段历史来比小事，很伤人。

Nazism
/ˈnɑːtsɪzəm/
核心：纳粹主义：希特勒及其政党的那套主张与统治，核心是种族主义、极端民族主义与独裁。不可数，首字母大写。
① The exhibition traces the rise of Nazism from the defeat of 1918 to the seizure of power.
= 那场展览追溯了纳粹主义的兴起，从一九一八年的战败讲到夺取政权。
the rise of Nazism = 纳粹主义的兴起
② Millions of people across Europe were murdered under Nazism.
= 在纳粹统治下，欧洲各地有数百万人遭到杀害。
under Nazism = 在纳粹统治下

ne'er
/neə(r)/
核心：两支。一是（诗歌、旧）never 的缩略形，只见于韵文与仿古的说法；
二是活在复合词 ne'er-do-well 里：没出息的人、游手好闲之徒，可数，旧式说法，带点宽容的取笑。
① "Ne'er shall I forget that shore," sang the sailor in the old ballad.
= 那首老歌谣里，水手唱道：「我永不会忘记那片海岸。」
ne'er = 永不（诗歌用语）
② Her uncle was a charming ne'er-do-well who borrowed money from every relative.
= 她叔叔是个讨人喜欢的二流子，跟每个亲戚都借过钱。
a ne'er-do-well = 没出息的人

Neanderthal
/niˈændətɑːl/
核心：两支。一是尼安德特人：冰河时期生活在欧洲的一种古人类，名词可数，也作定语；
二是（贬，口语）粗野、观念落后的人，这时首字母常小写。
① Neanderthals buried their dead and looked after the injured members of the group.
= 尼安德特人会埋葬死者，也会照料群体里受伤的成员。
a Neanderthal = 尼安德特人
② A Neanderthal skull was found in the cave beside tools made of flint.
= 洞里发现了一个尼安德特人的头骨，旁边是燧石做的工具。
a Neanderthal skull = 尼安德特人头骨
③ She was tired of working for neanderthals who thought women could not read a balance sheet.
= 她受够了给一帮老古董干活，那些人以为女人看不懂资产负债表。
a neanderthal = 观念落后的粗人
这一支是骂人的话。

near
/nɪə(r)/
核心：介词、副词、形容词、动词四种用法，底子都是「离得不远」。比较级 nearer，最高级 nearest。
介词：空间上靠近，时间上接近，也说数量、状态接近（near tears、near the end）；也可以说 near to，意思相同。
形容词：近的；作定语时多用最高级（the nearest bank），原级多见于 in the near future、a near relative；
还指差一点就是的（a near disaster）、几乎一样的（the nearest thing to）。
副词：come near、draw near 是走近、临近；nowhere near 是远远不；near enough 是差不多；
加在形容词前构成复合词：near-perfect、near-impossible。动词是（正式）接近、靠近。
习语：a near miss 是险些相撞、差一点成功；a near thing 是险胜、险些出事；so near and yet so far 是功亏一篑。
名词 nearness 并入本条；near-death experience 是濒死体验，也并入。
① They bought a cottage near the sea and walk on the beach every morning.
= 他们在海边买了座小屋，每天早上去沙滩散步。
near the sea = 靠近海
② Is there a post office near here that stays open on Saturdays?
= 这附近有没有星期六照常营业的邮局？
near here = 这附近
③ Do not sit too near to the fire in that woollen dress.
= 穿着那条羊毛裙，别坐得离火太近。
near to something = 离某物近
④ The roads are always busy near the end of the month.
= 快到月底的时候，路上总是很堵。
near the end of something = 快到某事的结尾
⑤ She was near to tears when the last train pulled out without her.
= 末班车没等她就开走了，她快要哭出来。
near to tears = 快要哭出来
⑥ Where is the nearest chemist that is open at this hour?
= 这个钟点还开着的药店，最近的在哪儿？
the nearest something = 最近的…
⑦ The firm plans to open a second factory in the near future.
= 那家公司打算在不久的将来开第二家工厂。
in the near future = 在不久的将来
⑧ Only near relatives were invited to the funeral.
= 只有近亲受邀参加葬礼。
a near relative = 近亲
⑨ The opening night was a near disaster, with half the lights failing.
= 首演之夜差一点砸了，一半的灯都不亮。
a near disaster = 差一点就是灾难
⑩ This cabin is the nearest thing to a home that he has ever had.
= 这间小木屋，是他有过的最接近家的地方。
the nearest thing to something = 最接近某物的东西
⑪ The children fell silent as the day of the exam drew near.
= 考试的日子一天天临近，孩子们安静了下来。
draw near = 临近
⑫ Do not come any nearer, because the ice is thin by the bank.
= 别再走近了，岸边的冰很薄。
come near = 走近
⑬ The work is nowhere near finished, and the guests arrive on Friday.
= 活儿远远没干完，客人星期五就到。
nowhere near = 远远不
⑭ There were five hundred people there, or near enough.
= 那儿有五百人，或者说差不多这个数。
near enough = 差不多
⑮ The two paintings are near-identical, and only an expert can tell them apart.
= 那两幅画几乎一模一样，只有行家分得出来。
near-identical = 几乎一模一样的
near-perfect = 近乎完美的
⑯ The ship was nearing the harbour when the engine failed.
= 那艘船快到港口的时候，发动机坏了。
near something = 接近某处
这里是动词，偏正式。
⑰ She is nearing retirement and has started to hand over her cases.
= 她快退休了，已经开始移交手上的案子。
near retirement = 临近退休
⑱ Two planes had a near miss over the airport in thick cloud.
= 两架飞机在浓云里险些在机场上空相撞。
a near miss = 险些相撞
⑲ We caught the ferry in the end, but it was a near thing.
= 我们最后赶上了渡船，可是好险。
a near thing = 好险的事
⑳ He led the race until the final bend: so near and yet so far.
= 他一路领跑到最后一个弯道，真是功亏一篑。
so near and yet so far = 功亏一篑
㉑ The nearness of the school was the main reason they chose the house.
= 离学校近，是他们挑中那座房子的主要原因。
the nearness of something = 离某物近
㉒ He described a near-death experience in which he floated above the operating table.
= 他讲述了一次濒死体验：他飘在手术台的上方。
a near-death experience = 濒死体验

nearby
/ˌnɪəˈbaɪ/
核心：两支。一是形容词，附近的，只作定语，放在名词前；二是副词，在附近，放在动词后或句末。
跟 near 的分别：near 后面要接地点，这个词后面不接宾语。
① The injured walkers were taken to a nearby hospital by helicopter.
= 受伤的徒步者被直升机送到了附近的一家医院。
a nearby hospital = 附近的医院
② Her parents live nearby and collect the children from school twice a week.
= 她父母住在附近，每周两次去学校接孩子。
live nearby = 住在附近
这里是副词，后面不接宾语。

nearly
/ˈnɪəli/
核心：副词，几乎、差不多：差一点但还没到。跟 almost 大体通用，但不能放在 no、nobody、nothing、never 前面
（要说 almost nothing）。not nearly 是远远不、差得远：not nearly enough。
very nearly 与 pretty nearly 是加强的说法。
① It took nearly three hours to drive forty miles in the snow.
= 在雪里开四十英里，花了将近三个钟头。
nearly three hours = 将近三个钟头
② I nearly missed the flight because the taxi went to the wrong terminal.
= 出租车开错了航站楼，我差点误了航班。
nearly miss something = 差点错过某事
③ She very nearly drowned as a child and has feared deep water ever since.
= 她小时候差一点淹死，从此怕深水。
very nearly = 差一点就
④ There is not nearly enough bread for twenty people.
= 给二十个人吃，面包远远不够。
not nearly enough = 远远不够
not nearly = 远远不
⑤ The bottle is nearly empty, so put olive oil on the shopping list.
= 瓶子快空了，把橄榄油记到购物单上吧。
nearly empty = 快空了

nearside
/ˈnɪəsaɪd/
核心：两支，英式，驾驶用语。一是形容词，（车辆）靠路边一侧的，在英国即左侧，只作定语；
二是名词，the nearside，车的这一侧。反义 offside。
① The nearside mirror was knocked off by a van parked too close to the kerb.
= 靠路边那一侧的后视镜，被一辆紧贴路沿停着的货车撞掉了。
the nearside mirror = 靠路边一侧的后视镜
② There is a long scratch along the nearside of the car.
= 车子靠路边的那一侧有一道长长的划痕。
the nearside = 靠路边的一侧

nearsighted
/ˌnɪəˈsaɪtɪd/
核心：两支，主要是美式，英式说 short-sighted。一是近视的；二是（比喻）目光短浅的。
名词 nearsightedness 并入本条。
① He is so nearsighted that he cannot recognise his own wife across the street.
= 他近视得厉害，隔着一条街认不出自己的妻子。
be nearsighted = 近视的
② Cutting the training budget was a nearsighted way to save money.
= 砍掉培训预算，是一种目光短浅的省钱办法。
a nearsighted way = 目光短浅的办法
③ Her nearsightedness was not noticed until she failed to read the board at school.
= 直到她在学校看不清黑板，才有人发现她近视。
nearsightedness = 近视

neat
/niːt/
核心：五支。一是整洁的、整齐的：neat and tidy；二是（人）爱整洁的、有条理的；三是（做法、说法）简洁巧妙的；
四是（酒）不掺水、不加冰的，英式说法，美式说 straight；五是（美式口语）很棒的。
副词 neatly 与名词 neatness 并入本条。
① She keeps her desk neat and tidy, with every pencil pointing the same way.
= 她把书桌收拾得整整齐齐，每支铅笔都朝一个方向。
neat and tidy = 整整齐齐
② He has small, neat handwriting that fits forty lines on a page.
= 他的字又小又工整，一页能写四十行。
neat handwriting = 工整的字
③ He is a neat worker who sweeps up before he leaves the site.
= 他干活利落，离开工地前总要扫干净。
a neat worker = 干活利落的人
④ It was a neat solution to a problem that had defeated three engineers.
= 那是个巧妙的办法，解决了难倒三位工程师的问题。
a neat solution = 巧妙的解决办法
⑤ He drinks his whisky neat and frowns at anyone who adds ice.
= 他喝威士忌什么都不加，谁往里加冰他就皱眉头。
drink whisky neat = 喝纯的威士忌
这一支是英式说法。
⑥ "We are going to the lake tomorrow." "That sounds really neat."
= 「我们明天去湖边。」「听起来真不错。」
neat = 很棒的（美式口语）
⑦ The shirts were neatly folded and stacked according to colour.
= 衬衫叠得整整齐齐，按颜色码放。
neatly = 整齐地
neatly folded = 叠得整整齐齐
⑧ The title neatly sums up the argument of the whole book.
= 那个书名把全书的论点概括得恰到好处。
neatly sum up = 概括得恰到好处
⑨ The teacher gave extra marks for the neatness of the diagrams.
= 老师因为图画得整洁，多给了分。
the neatness of something = 某物的整洁

neaten
/ˈniːtən/
核心：把…弄整齐、理一理。及物动词，常说 neaten something up。对象多是头发、衣服、纸张、边角。
① She neatened her hair in the lift mirror before the meeting.
= 开会之前，她对着电梯里的镜子理了理头发。
neaten your hair = 理一理头发
② He neatened up the pile of papers and squared it against the edge of the desk.
= 他把那叠文件理齐，靠着桌边码正。
neaten something up = 把某物理整齐

nebbish
/ˈnebɪʃ/
核心：两支，口语，来自意第绪语，主要是美式。一是名词，窝囊废、胆小没用的人，可数；二是形容词，窝囊的。
① The hero of the comedy is a nebbish who apologises to people who tread on his feet.
= 那出喜剧的主角是个窝囊废，别人踩了他的脚，他反倒道歉。
a nebbish = 窝囊废
② He plays a nebbish accountant who is mistaken for a spy.
= 他演一个被错当成间谍的窝囊会计。
a nebbish accountant = 窝囊的会计

nebula
/ˈnebjʊlə/
核心：星云：太空里由气体和尘埃组成的云状天体，有的发光，有的是暗的。可数，复数 nebulae 或 nebulas。
① The telescope picked out a nebula glowing red in the arm of the galaxy.
= 望远镜在星系的旋臂上找到了一团发着红光的星云。
a nebula = 星云
② Stars are born inside nebulae when the gas collapses under its own weight.
= 气体在自身重量下塌缩时，恒星就在星云内部诞生。
inside nebulae = 在星云内部

nebulizer
/ˈnebjʊlaɪzə(r)/
核心：雾化器：把药液变成细雾、让病人吸进肺里的装置，治哮喘等病用。可数，英式也拼作 nebuliser。
① The nurse set up a nebulizer and the boy's breathing eased within minutes.
= 护士装好雾化器，那个男孩的呼吸几分钟内就顺畅了。
a nebulizer = 雾化器
② She uses a nebulizer twice a day during the winter months.
= 冬天那几个月，她每天用两次雾化器。
use a nebulizer = 用雾化器

nebulous
/ˈnebjʊləs/
核心：两支，正式。一是（想法、概念、计划）模糊的、含混不清的；二是（形状）朦胧的、轮廓不清的。
① The manifesto offers only a nebulous promise to improve public services.
= 那份竞选纲领只给了一个含混的承诺，说要改善公共服务。
a nebulous promise = 含混的承诺
② His plans for the business were still too nebulous to show to a bank.
= 他对那桩生意的打算还太模糊，拿不出手给银行看。
be too nebulous = 太模糊
③ A nebulous shape moved in the mist at the far end of the field.
= 田地尽头的雾里，有个朦胧的影子在动。
a nebulous shape = 朦胧的影子

necessary
/ˈnesəsəri/
核心：两支。一是必要的、必需的：it is necessary (for somebody) to do something、if necessary、
as necessary（按需要）、when necessary；介词用 for。二是（正式）必然的、不可避免的，只作定语：a necessary consequence。
a necessary evil 是不得不忍受的坏事。反义 unnecessary。
副词 necessarily 是必然地，最常见于 not necessarily（未必）；名词 necessaries 是（旧）必需品，复数。并入本条。
① It is necessary to book a week ahead during the summer holidays.
= 暑假期间有必要提前一个星期预订。
It is necessary to do = 有必要做…
② I can stay until Friday if necessary, but I would rather leave on Thursday.
= 必要的话我可以待到星期五，不过我更想星期四走。
if necessary = 必要的话
③ Add a little more water as necessary until the dough holds together.
= 按需要再加一点水，直到面团能成形。
as necessary = 按需要
④ She has all the skills necessary for the job and ten years of experience.
= 这份工作所需的技能她都有，还有十年经验。
necessary for something = 某事所必需的
⑤ Most people regard taxes as a necessary evil.
= 多数人把税看成一种不得不忍受的坏事。
a necessary evil = 不得不忍受的坏事
⑥ Higher prices are a necessary consequence of a poor harvest.
= 收成不好，价格上涨是必然的结果。
a necessary consequence = 必然的结果
这一支偏正式，指逻辑上免不了。
⑦ The meeting ran on for an hour of unnecessary detail.
= 那场会拖了一个钟头，净是些不必要的细节。
unnecessary = 不必要的
⑧ The most expensive wine on the list is not necessarily the best.
= 酒单上最贵的酒，未必是最好的。
not necessarily = 未必
⑨ A bigger budget does not necessarily mean a better film.
= 预算大，不一定片子就好。
necessarily = 必然地
⑩ He packed the necessaries for a week in a single small bag.
= 他把一个星期的必需品装进了一只小包。
the necessaries = 必需品

necessitate
/nəˈsesɪteɪt/
核心：（正式）使成为必要、使不得不：主语是原因，宾语是不得不做的事。后面接名词或动名词，不接不定式。
① The flood damage necessitated the closure of the school for a month.
= 洪水造成的损坏，使学校不得不关闭一个月。
necessitate something = 使某事成为必要
② The move to a smaller office necessitated selling half the furniture.
= 搬进小一些的办公室，就不得不卖掉一半家具。
necessitate doing something = 使不得不做某事
后面接动名词。

necessity
/nəˈsesɪti/
核心：三支。一是必要、必要性，不可数，介词用 for、of；out of necessity、from necessity 是迫不得已。
二是必需品，可数，跟 luxury 相对：the basic necessities。三是必然发生的事，单数。
谚语 necessity is the mother of invention 是「需要是发明之母」；of necessity 是（正式）必然地。
① There is no necessity for anyone to work late tonight.
= 今晚谁都没有必要加班。
the necessity for something = 某事的必要
② She took a second job out of necessity when the rent went up.
= 房租一涨，她迫不得已找了第二份工作。
out of necessity = 迫不得已
③ Clean water is a basic necessity that the camp still lacks.
= 干净的水是基本必需品，那个营地至今还缺。
a basic necessity = 基本必需品
necessity = 必需品
④ A car is a necessity rather than a luxury in a village with two buses a day.
= 在一天只有两班公共汽车的村子里，汽车是必需品，不是奢侈品。
a necessity rather than a luxury = 是必需品而非奢侈品
⑤ Having no oven, she invented a way to bake bread in a pan: necessity is the mother of invention.
= 没有烤箱，她就想出了用平底锅烤面包的办法：需要是发明之母。
necessity is the mother of invention = 需要是发明之母
⑥ The survey was, of necessity, limited to those who could be reached by telephone.
= 那项调查势必只限于电话联系得上的人。
of necessity = 必然地、势必

neck
/nek/
核心：名词四支。一是脖子、颈部；二是衣领、领口：a V-neck、a polo neck；三是（瓶子、乐器、陆地的）细长部分：
the neck of a bottle；四是作食物的颈肉。动词是（口语，旧）搂着脖子亲吻。
习语很多：neck and neck 是并驾齐驱；up to your neck in 是深陷于、忙得不可开交；breathe down somebody's neck 是盯得很紧；
stick your neck out 是冒险出头；a pain in the neck 是讨厌的人或事；risk your neck 是冒生命危险；
by a neck 是（赛马）以一颈之差，引申为险胜；neck of the woods 是（口语）这一带、那一带；
get it in the neck 是（英式）挨一顿狠批。
① She wore a silk scarf round her neck to hide the scar.
= 她脖子上围了条丝巾，遮住那道疤。
round your neck = 围在脖子上
② He woke with a stiff neck after sleeping in the armchair.
= 他在扶手椅上睡了一觉，醒来脖子僵了。
a stiff neck = 脖子僵硬
③ The giraffe stretched its long neck to reach the leaves at the top.
= 长颈鹿伸长脖子，去够顶上的树叶。
a long neck = 长脖子
④ The jumper has a round neck that is too tight for him.
= 那件套头衫是圆领的，他穿着太紧。
a round neck = 圆领
a V-neck = 鸡心领
⑤ A cork was stuck in the neck of the bottle and would not come out.
= 一个软木塞卡在瓶颈里，怎么也弄不出来。
the neck of a bottle = 瓶颈
⑥ The stew is made with neck of lamb, which is cheap and full of flavour.
= 那道炖菜用的是羊颈肉，便宜，味道又足。
neck of lamb = 羊颈肉
⑦ The two runners were neck and neck until the last twenty metres.
= 两名选手一直并驾齐驱，直到最后二十米。
neck and neck = 并驾齐驱
⑧ I am up to my neck in paperwork until the end of the tax year.
= 税务年度结束之前，我都埋在文书堆里。
up to your neck in = 深陷于
up to your neck in something = 被某事缠得脱不开身
⑨ I cannot work with the manager breathing down my neck all afternoon.
= 经理一下午盯在我背后，我没法干活。
breathe down somebody's neck = 盯得很紧
⑩ He stuck his neck out and told the board that the plan would fail.
= 他冒险出头，告诉董事会那个计划会失败。
stick your neck out = 冒险出头
⑪ Filling in the same form three times is a real pain in the neck.
= 同一张表要填三遍，真烦人。
a pain in the neck = 讨厌的人或事
⑫ I am not going to risk my neck climbing that roof in the rain.
= 我才不冒着摔死的危险，在雨里爬那个屋顶。
risk your neck = 冒生命危险
⑬ The favourite won by a neck after trailing for most of the race.
= 那匹热门马落后了大半程，最后以一颈之差获胜。
by a neck = 以一颈之差
⑭ What brings you to this neck of the woods on a Sunday?
= 星期天的，什么风把你吹到这一带来了？
neck of the woods = 这一带
⑮ The junior staff got it in the neck when the figures turned out to be wrong.
= 数字一查出来是错的，挨狠批的是基层员工。
get it in the neck = 挨一顿狠批
⑯ A couple of teenagers were necking in the back row of the cinema.
= 电影院后排有一对少男少女在搂着亲吻。
neck = 搂着亲吻
旧式口语。

neckerchief
/ˈnekətʃiːf/
核心：领巾、围在脖子上的方巾，可数。多说童子军、水手、牛仔戴的那种，如今听着有点旧。
① The scouts wore green neckerchiefs held in place by a leather ring.
= 童子军戴着绿色领巾，用一个皮环箍住。
a neckerchief = 领巾
② The farmer wiped his face with the red neckerchief he wore in the fields.
= 那个农夫用他下地时戴的红领巾擦了把脸。
a red neckerchief = 红领巾

necklace
/ˈnekləs/
核心：项链：戴在脖子上的一串珠宝、珠子或链子。可数。
necklacing 指把灌了汽油的轮胎套在人脖子上点燃的私刑，见于对南非种族隔离末期的报道，并入本条。
① She wore her grandmother's pearl necklace on her wedding day.
= 婚礼那天，她戴着祖母的珍珠项链。
a pearl necklace = 珍珠项链
necklace = 项链
② The clasp of the necklace broke and the beads rolled all over the floor.
= 项链的搭扣断了，珠子滚了一地。
the clasp of a necklace = 项链的搭扣
③ The report documents killings by necklacing in the townships during the nineteen-eighties.
= 那份报告记录了二十世纪八十年代黑人城镇里用轮胎火刑杀人的事件。
necklacing = 轮胎火刑

neckline
/ˈneklaɪn/
核心：领口、领线：女装上衣领口的形状与高低。可数。常搭 a low neckline、a plunging neckline、a high neckline。
① The dress has a low neckline that her mother thought unsuitable for church.
= 那条裙子领口很低，她母亲觉得穿去教堂不合适。
a low neckline = 低领口
② A high neckline and long sleeves keep the sun off on the boat.
= 高领口加长袖，在船上能挡太阳。
a high neckline = 高领口
a plunging neckline = 深 V 领口

necktie
/ˈnektaɪ/
核心：领带，可数。美式里是正式说法，日常英美都只说 tie；英式里这个词听着旧。
① The restaurant requires gentlemen to wear a jacket and necktie after six.
= 那家餐厅要求男士六点以后穿外套、打领带。
wear a necktie = 打领带
② He loosened his necktie as soon as the photographers had left the room.
= 摄影记者一走出房间，他就松开了领带。
loosen your necktie = 松开领带

neckwear
/ˈnekweə(r)/
核心：颈部服饰的总称：领带、领结、围巾之类。不可数，是商店与行业的用词，日常不这么说。
① The shop sells shirts on the ground floor and neckwear on the first.
= 那家店一楼卖衬衫，二楼卖领带围巾。
neckwear = 领带围巾类
② His taste in neckwear ran to bow ties with spots.
= 他在领饰上的口味，偏好带圆点的领结。
somebody's taste in neckwear = 某人在领饰上的口味

necromancy
/ˈnekrəʊmænsi/
核心：两支，不可数。一是招魂术：召唤死者的亡灵来预卜未来的巫术；二是泛指妖术、黑巫术。
施术者叫 necromancer，并入本条。
① The king was accused of using necromancy to learn the outcome of the battle.
= 有人指控那位国王用招魂术打探战事的结局。
use necromancy = 用招魂术
② In the game you play a necromancer who raises an army of skeletons.
= 在那款游戏里，你扮演一个召唤骷髅大军的亡灵巫师。
a necromancer = 亡灵巫师

necrophilia
/ˌnekrəˈfɪliə/
核心：两支，不可数。一是恋尸癖：对尸体有性方面的迷恋，精神医学与法律用语；二是（比喻，贬）对死亡、衰亡之物的病态迷恋。
有这种癖好的人叫 necrophiliac，并入本条。
① The court heard that the accused had a history of necrophilia.
= 法庭获悉，被告有恋尸癖的前科。
a history of necrophilia = 恋尸癖的前科
② The critic called the revival of the old style a kind of cultural necrophilia.
= 那位评论家把那种旧风格的复兴说成一种文化上的恋尸癖。
cultural necrophilia = 文化上对死物的迷恋
③ The novel is told by a necrophiliac who works in a city mortuary.
= 那部小说的讲述者是个在市立停尸房工作的恋尸癖者。
a necrophiliac = 恋尸癖者

necropolis
/nəˈkrɒpəlɪs/
核心：两支，可数。一是（古代城市的）大墓地；二是（书面）规模很大的公墓。复数 necropolises。
① Archaeologists have uncovered a necropolis of two thousand tombs outside the old city walls.
= 考古学家在旧城墙外发掘出一处有两千座墓的古墓地。
a necropolis = 古代大墓地
② The Victorian necropolis on the hill has its own chapel and railway station.
= 山上那座维多利亚时代的大公墓，自带礼拜堂和火车站。
a Victorian necropolis = 维多利亚时代的大公墓

necropsy
/ˈnekrɒpsi/
核心：两支，可数，术语。一是尸体剖检：多指对动物尸体做的检验，对人一般说 autopsy 或 post-mortem；二是动词，对…做剖检。
① The vet carried out a necropsy on the calf to find the cause of death.
= 兽医给那头小牛做了剖检，以查明死因。
carry out a necropsy = 做尸体剖检
② All the birds found on the beach were necropsied at the university.
= 海滩上发现的那些鸟，全部送到大学做了剖检。
be necropsied = 被剖检

necrosis
/neˈkrəʊsɪs/
核心：坏死：身体里一部分组织或细胞因伤病、缺血而死亡。不可数，医学用语。
病名 necrotizing fasciitis 是坏死性筋膜炎，即俗称的「食肉菌」感染，并入本条。
① Frostbite caused necrosis in two of his toes, and both were removed.
= 冻伤造成他两个脚趾坏死，两个都截掉了。
cause necrosis = 造成坏死
② She almost died of necrotizing fasciitis after a small cut became infected.
= 一个小伤口感染之后，她差点死于坏死性筋膜炎。
necrotizing fasciitis = 坏死性筋膜炎

nectar
/ˈnektə(r)/
核心：三支，不可数。一是花蜜：花分泌的甜液，蜜蜂采来酿蜜；二是（希腊神话）众神喝的琼浆，引申指极好喝的饮料；
三是浓稠的果汁饮料。
① Bees fly from flower to flower collecting nectar to carry back to the hive.
= 蜜蜂从一朵花飞到另一朵花，采集花蜜带回蜂巢。
collect nectar = 采花蜜
② After six hours on the hill, a cup of sweet tea tasted like nectar.
= 在山上走了六个钟头，一杯甜茶喝起来就像琼浆。
taste like nectar = 喝起来像琼浆
③ The hotel serves peach nectar with breakfast instead of orange juice.
= 那家旅馆早餐配的是浓桃汁，不是橙汁。
peach nectar = 浓桃汁

nectarine
/ˈnektəriːn/
核心：两支，可数。一是油桃：桃的一个变种，果皮光滑无毛；二是结这种果子的树。
① She bit into a ripe nectarine and the juice ran down her chin.
= 她咬了一口熟透的油桃，汁水顺着下巴往下流。
a ripe nectarine = 熟透的油桃
② The nectarine against the south wall fruits well in a warm summer.
= 靠南墙的那棵油桃树，夏天暖和的年份结果很好。
a nectarine = 油桃树

nee
/neɪ/
核心：形容词，来自法语，也写作 née。放在已婚女子的夫姓后面、娘家姓前面，意思是「娘家姓…」。多见于讣告、族谱与正式介绍。
① The book is dedicated to Mary Turner, nee Hobbs, who typed every page.
= 那本书题献给玛丽·特纳——娘家姓霍布斯——每一页都是她打的字。
nee Hobbs = 娘家姓霍布斯
② The notice announced the death of Mrs Anne Carr, nee Wilson, at the age of ninety.
= 那则启事宣告安妮·卡尔太太去世，她娘家姓威尔逊，享年九十岁。
Mrs Carr, nee Wilson = 卡尔太太，娘家姓威尔逊

need
/niːd/
核心：动词、情态动词、名词三种身份。
实义动词：需要；need to do something 是需要做、必须做；need doing 等于 need to be done（东西需要被…）；
否定与疑问照常用 do：do not need to、Do you need…?
情态动词（英式，偏正式）：只用于否定句与疑问句，没有人称变化，后面接不带 to 的原形：need not（缩写 needn't）是不必；
needn't have done 是本来不必做却做了，跟 did not need to（不必做，也没做）不同。
名词：需要、必要，介词用 for、of；in need of 是需要；there is no need to do 是没必要；
if need be 是必要的话；in need 是处于贫困与困难之中；复数 needs 是各项需求。
副词 needs 只见于旧式的 must needs（非得、偏要）。
need-to-know 指只让确有必要知道的人知道；need-blind 是美式说法，指（大学）录取时不看学生家境。并入本条。
① You need a passport and a visa to cross the border at that point.
= 从那个地点过境，需要护照和签证。
need something = 需要某物
② I need to renew the insurance before the end of the week.
= 我得在这个星期结束前把保险续上。
need to do = 需要做某事
③ You do not need to bring anything, because lunch is provided.
= 你什么都不用带，午饭是供应的。
do not need to do something = 不必做某事
否定 = 不必
④ The windows need cleaning, and the gutters have to be cleared as well.
= 窗户该擦了，檐沟也得清一清。
need doing = 需要被…
主语是东西，后面的动名词表示被动。
⑤ He needed his eyes tested long before he admitted it.
= 早在他肯承认之前，他就该去验光了。
need something done = 需要让人把某事做了
⑥ You need not worry, because the dog is far too old to chase anyone.
= 你不必担心，那条狗太老了，追不动人。
need not do something = 不必做某事
这里是情态动词，后面不带 to。
⑦ You needn't have bought flowers, though they are lovely.
= 你本来不用买花的，不过这花真好看。
needn't have done = 本来不必做（却做了）
needn't = need not 的缩写
⑧ Need I say more about a hotel that charges for hot water?
= 一家连热水都要收费的旅馆，还用我多说吗？
Need I say more? = 还用我多说吗？
⑨ There is an urgent need for nurses in the rural clinics.
= 乡村诊所急需护士。
a need for something = 对某物的需要
⑩ The roof is badly in need of repair after last winter's storms.
= 经过去年冬天的几场风暴，屋顶亟待修理。
in need of = 需要
in need of repair = 需要修理
⑪ There is no need to shout, because I can hear you perfectly well.
= 没必要嚷嚷，我听得清清楚楚。
no need = 没必要
there is no need to do something = 没必要做某事
⑫ I can drive you to the airport myself if need be.
= 必要的话，我可以亲自开车送你去机场。
if need be = 必要的话
⑬ The charity gives food and clothing to families in need.
= 那家慈善机构给困难家庭提供食物和衣服。
in need = 处于贫困中
⑭ The school tries to meet the needs of children with hearing problems.
= 那所学校设法满足听力有障碍的孩子的需求。
meet the needs of somebody = 满足某人的需求
needs = 各项需求
⑮ He had a perfectly good coat, but he must needs buy another.
= 他明明有件好好的大衣，偏要再买一件。
must needs = 非得、偏要
旧式说法，如今多带嘲讽。
⑯ Details of the route are given out strictly on a need-to-know basis.
= 路线的细节严格按「有必要才告知」的原则透露。
need-to-know = 只告知确有必要知道的人
on a need-to-know basis = 按有必要才告知的原则
⑰ Only a handful of American colleges are need-blind for students from abroad.
= 对外国学生录取时不看家境的美国大学，只有寥寥几所。
need-blind = 录取时不看家境的

needful
/ˈniːdfəl/
核心：两支，旧式或正式。一是形容词，必需的、必要的；二是 do the needful，把该办的事办了，这个说法如今多见于南亚英语的公文信函。
① He packed only what was needful for a journey of three days.
= 他只带了三天路程所必需的东西。
what is needful = 必需的东西
② Please send the form to the bank and ask them to do the needful.
= 请把表格寄给银行，让他们照章办理。
do the needful = 把该办的事办了

needle
/ˈniːdəl/
核心：名词六支。一是缝衣针；二是毛衣针：knitting needles；三是注射针头；四是（仪表、指南针的）指针；
五是（松树等的）针叶：pine needles；六是唱机的唱针。
动词是（口语）故意拿话刺人、惹人恼。习语 a needle in a haystack 是大海捞针。
① She threaded the needle at the first attempt and sewed on the button.
= 她一下就把线穿过了针眼，把扣子缝上了。
thread a needle = 穿针
needle = 针
② My grandmother's knitting needles clicked all through the evening news.
= 整个晚间新闻的时间里，我奶奶的毛衣针咔嗒咔嗒响个不停。
knitting needles = 毛衣针
③ The nurse changed the needle and took a second sample of blood.
= 护士换了针头，又抽了一份血样。
change the needle = 换针头
④ The needle on the fuel gauge was already touching the red line.
= 油表的指针已经碰到红线了。
the needle on a gauge = 仪表的指针
⑤ The path through the wood was soft with fallen pine needles.
= 穿过树林的小路上落满松针，踩上去软软的。
pine needles = 松针
⑥ He lowered the needle onto the record and the room filled with jazz.
= 他把唱针放到唱片上，屋里顿时响起爵士乐。
lower the needle = 放下唱针
⑦ Finding one receipt in these boxes is like looking for a needle in a haystack.
= 在这些箱子里找一张收据，简直是大海捞针。
a needle in a haystack = 大海捞针
⑧ His sister kept needling him about his exam results until he left the table.
= 他姐姐不停地拿考试成绩刺他，直到他离开饭桌。
needle somebody about something = 拿某事刺某人

needlecord
/ˈniːdəlkɔːd/
核心：两支，英式，不可数。一是细条灯芯绒：绒条很细的灯芯绒面料；二是作定语，用这种面料做的。
① The dress is made of soft needlecord that does not crease in a suitcase.
= 那条裙子用柔软的细条灯芯绒做成，放在行李箱里不起皱。
made of needlecord = 细条灯芯绒做的
② He wore a brown needlecord jacket with patches on the elbows.
= 他穿着一件肘部打了补丁的棕色细条灯芯绒上衣。
a needlecord jacket = 细条灯芯绒上衣

needlepoint
/ˈniːdəlpɔɪnt/
核心：两支，不可数。一是针绣：在网眼布上用毛线一针针绣满图案的手工，做靠垫、椅面；二是这样绣出来的成品。
① She took up needlepoint to keep her hands busy after giving up smoking.
= 她戒烟以后学起了针绣，好让手上有事做。
take up needlepoint = 学起针绣
② The chairs in the hall are covered in needlepoint worked by her great-aunt.
= 门厅里那几把椅子的椅面，是她姑婆绣的针绣。
covered in needlepoint = 蒙着针绣

needless
/ˈniːdləs/
核心：不必要的、本可避免的：多说痛苦、死亡、开销、延误。needless to say 是不用说、自然，作插入语。
副词 needlessly 并入本条。
① The report says that poor planning caused needless suffering in the camps.
= 报告说，计划不周在营地里造成了本可避免的苦难。
needless suffering = 本可避免的苦难
② Needless to say, the one day we forgot the umbrella was the day it poured.
= 不用说，我们唯一忘带伞的那天，偏偏下了倾盆大雨。
needless to say = 不用说
③ Hundreds of patients are kept waiting needlessly because of a shortage of clerks.
= 因为文员不够，成百上千的病人白白地等着。
wait needlessly = 白白地等

needlework
/ˈniːdəlwɜːk/
核心：针线活、女红：缝纫、刺绣这类用针的手工，也指做出来的活计。不可数。
needlecraft 意思相同，偏指这门手艺；做针线的女子旧称 needlewoman。并入本条。
① The girls were taught needlework while the boys did woodwork.
= 女孩子学针线，男孩子做木工。
be taught needlework = 学针线
② The museum has a fine collection of needlework from the seventeenth century.
= 那座博物馆收藏着一批十七世纪的精美绣品。
a collection of needlework = 一批绣品
③ The magazine is aimed at people who enjoy knitting and other kinds of needlecraft.
= 那本杂志面向喜欢编织和别的针线手艺的人。
needlecraft = 针线手艺
④ Her mother was a skilled needlewoman who made all the family's clothes.
= 她母亲针线活做得很好，全家人的衣服都是她做的。
a needlewoman = 做针线的女子

needy
/ˈniːdi/
核心：两支。一是贫困的、缺衣少食的：the needy 指穷人这个群体；二是（情感上）缺乏安全感、老要人关注与安慰的，略贬。
① The fund pays for school meals for children from needy families.
= 那笔基金给贫困家庭的孩子出校餐费。
needy families = 贫困家庭
② The church collects warm clothes for the needy every autumn.
= 那座教堂每年秋天为穷人募集保暖的衣服。
the needy = 穷人
③ He was a needy child who followed his mother from room to room.
= 他是个黏人的孩子，母亲走到哪个房间他跟到哪个房间。
a needy child = 黏人的孩子
这一支说的是情感上离不开人。

neem
/niːm/
核心：两支。一是印楝：南亚的一种常绿乔木，可数；二是从它的叶子、种子里提取的东西，用来驱虫、入药，不可数。
① A neem stands at the centre of the village and shades the well.
= 村子中央有一棵印楝树，遮着那口井。
a neem = 印楝树
② Gardeners spray neem oil on roses to keep the greenfly away.
= 园丁往玫瑰上喷印楝油驱蚜虫。
neem oil = 印楝油

nefarious
/nɪˈfeəriəs/
核心：两支，正式。一是（行为、计划）邪恶的、不法的；二是（诙谐）不可告人的，拿这个大词说小事。只作定语居多。
① The company was accused of using customers' data for nefarious purposes.
= 那家公司被指控把客户数据用于不法目的。
nefarious purposes = 不法目的
② The children were whispering in the kitchen about some nefarious plan to stay up late.
= 孩子们在厨房里嘀嘀咕咕，商量着某个不可告人的熬夜计划。
a nefarious plan = 不可告人的计划
这里是玩笑话。

negate
/nɪˈɡeɪt/
核心：两支，正式。一是使无效、抵消掉：让先前的努力、好处化为乌有；二是否定、否认某事存在；语法上指把句子变成否定。
名词 negation 并入本条。
① One careless remark negated months of patient diplomacy.
= 一句不小心的话，让几个月耐心的外交努力化为乌有。
negate something = 使某事化为乌有
② The extra fuel the heavier car burns negates any saving on the price.
= 那辆较重的车多烧的油，把价钱上省下的钱全抵消了。
negate a saving = 抵消省下的钱
③ In English you negate a verb by adding "not" after the auxiliary.
= 英语里否定一个动词，是在助动词后面加否定词。
negate a verb = 否定一个动词
④ The decision was a negation of everything the charity stood for.
= 那个决定否定了那家慈善机构所主张的一切。
a negation of something = 对某事的否定

negative
/ˈneɡətɪv/
核心：形容词六支。一是负面的、有害的：a negative effect；二是消极的、悲观的，介词用 about；
三是否定的、表示拒绝的：a negative answer；四是（化验结果）阴性的，介词用 for；
五是（数）负的：a negative number；六是（电）负极的。
名词一是否定的回答、否定词：answer in the negative；二是照片的底片；三是不利的一面，跟 positive 相对。
double negative 是双重否定。副词 negatively 与名词 negativity（消极情绪）并入本条。
negative equity 指房子的市价跌到低于所欠房贷，并入本条。
① The strike had a negative effect on sales for the rest of the year.
= 那次罢工给当年余下几个月的销售带来了负面影响。
a negative effect = 负面影响
② She has been very negative about the move since the day it was announced.
= 自从宣布搬迁的那天起，她一直很消极。
negative = 消极的
be negative about something = 对某事态度消极
③ We wrote twice to the council and received a negative reply each time.
= 我们给市政会写了两回信，每回得到的都是否定的答复。
a negative reply = 否定的答复
④ His test for the virus came back negative, and he returned to work.
= 他的病毒检测结果是阴性，于是回去上班了。
test negative = 检测呈阴性
negative for something = 某项检测为阴性
⑤ A negative number multiplied by another negative number gives a positive one.
= 负数乘负数得正数。
a negative number = 负数
⑥ Connect the black lead to the negative terminal of the battery.
= 把黑色导线接到电池的负极上。
the negative terminal = 负极
⑦ When asked whether he had seen the letter, he answered in the negative.
= 问他见没见过那封信，他作了否定的回答。
answer in the negative = 作否定的回答
⑧ She found the negatives of her parents' wedding photographs in a biscuit tin.
= 她在一只饼干罐里找到了父母结婚照的底片。
a negative = 底片
⑨ The only negative is the long drive to the nearest station.
= 唯一的不足，是到最近的车站要开很久的车。
a negative = 不利的一面
⑩ "I did not see nothing" is a double negative that teachers love to correct.
= 这种说法是双重否定，老师最爱纠正。
a double negative = 双重否定
⑪ The cuts will affect small schools more negatively than large ones.
= 那些削减对小学校的不利影响，会比对大学校的更大。
affect something negatively = 对某事有不利影响
⑫ The coach refused to allow any negativity in the dressing room before the final.
= 决赛之前，教练不许更衣室里有任何消极情绪。
negativity = 消极情绪
⑬ Thousands of homeowners were trapped in negative equity when prices fell.
= 房价一跌，成千上万的房主陷进了负资产。
negative equity = 负资产

negging
/ˈneɡɪŋ/
核心：两支，口语，不可数。一是用明褒暗贬的话打压对方自信、好让对方讨好自己的搭讪手法；
二是泛指这种夹枪带棒的「夸奖」。含贬义。
① The article warns teenagers to recognise negging and to walk away from it.
= 那篇文章提醒青少年要认得出这种打压式搭讪，掉头走开。
negging = 打压式搭讪
② "You are quite clever for someone so pretty" is a textbook case of negging.
= 「你这么漂亮，居然还挺聪明」——这是明褒暗贬的典型例子。
a case of negging = 一个明褒暗贬的例子

neglect
/nɪˈɡlekt/
核心：动词三支。一是疏于照管（孩子、动物、房子）；二是忽视、不重视；三是（正式）neglect to do something，没有做该做的事。
名词是疏于照管、忽视的状态，不可数：years of neglect、a state of neglect。
形容词 neglected（没人管的、被冷落的）与 neglectful（疏忽的，介词用 of）并入本条。
① The owner was fined for neglecting two horses left in a muddy field all winter.
= 那个主人把两匹马丢在泥地里一冬不管，被罚了款。
neglect an animal = 疏于照管动物
② She has been neglecting her studies since she joined the band.
= 自从加入乐队，她一直荒废学业。
neglect your studies = 荒废学业
③ He neglected to mention that the flat had no heating.
= 他没提那套公寓没有暖气。
neglect to do something = 没有做（该做的）某事
偏正式，暗示是该做的事。
④ The house fell into ruin after thirty years of neglect.
= 三十年无人照管，那座房子成了废墟。
years of neglect = 多年无人照管
⑤ The social workers found clear signs of child neglect in the home.
= 社工在那户人家发现了疏于照管孩子的明显迹象。
child neglect = 疏于照管孩子
⑥ The garden was neglected and overgrown, with brambles across the path.
= 花园没人打理，荒草丛生，小路上横着荆棘。
be neglected = 没人管
a neglected garden = 荒废的花园
⑦ She admitted that she had been neglectful of her own health for years.
= 她承认自己多年来疏忽了自己的身体。
be neglectful of something = 疏忽某事

negligee
/ˈneɡlɪʒeɪ/
核心：两支，可数，来自法语，也写作 negligée。一是女式薄睡袍：轻薄、带花边的那种；二是泛指这类贴身的家居衣着。
① She came down to breakfast in a silk negligee and slippers.
= 她穿着一件丝绸薄睡袍和拖鞋下楼吃早饭。
a silk negligee = 丝绸薄睡袍
② The shop window displayed a lace negligee at a price that made her laugh.
= 橱窗里摆着一件蕾丝睡袍，那价钱让她笑出了声。
a lace negligee = 蕾丝睡袍

negligence
/ˈneɡlɪdʒəns/
核心：疏忽、过失：没有尽到应有的小心，不可数。法律上指因此造成他人损害而要担责的行为：medical negligence。
形容词 negligent 一是疏忽的、失职的，二是（书面）漫不经心、随意的；副词 negligently。并入本条。
① The accident was caused by negligence on the part of the driver.
= 那起事故是司机一方的疏忽造成的。
negligence on the part of somebody = 某人一方的疏忽
② The family is suing the hospital for medical negligence.
= 那家人正以医疗过失为由起诉医院。
medical negligence = 医疗过失
③ The court found the school negligent in failing to supervise the pool.
= 法院认定，学校没有派人看管泳池，属于失职。
be negligent in doing something = 在做某事上失职
④ He waved a negligent hand towards the chairs and went on reading.
= 他漫不经心地朝椅子挥了挥手，接着看他的书。
a negligent hand = 漫不经心的一挥手
这一支书面，指随意、不上心的样子。
⑤ The guard had negligently left the gate unlocked overnight.
= 那个门卫疏忽大意，让大门一夜没锁。
negligently leave something = 疏忽大意地留下某物

negligible
/ˈneɡlɪdʒəbəl/
核心：两支。一是微不足道的、小到可以忽略的：多说数量、影响、风险、成本；二是（否定形式）not negligible，不容小看的。
① The cost of running the lamp for a year is negligible.
= 那盏灯开一年的花费微不足道。
be negligible = 微不足道
② The change had a negligible effect on the time it takes to cross the city.
= 那项改动对穿城所需的时间影响小到可以忽略。
a negligible effect = 可以忽略的影响
③ The risk to swimmers is small but not negligible.
= 对游泳的人来说，风险不大，但也不容小看。
not negligible = 不容小看

negotiate
/nɪˈɡəʊʃieɪt/
核心：三支。一是谈判、协商：不及物，介词 with 接对方、for 或 over 接要谈的事；二是通过谈判达成，及物：negotiate a deal；
三是顺利通过（难走的路段、障碍）。
形容词 negotiable 一是可以商量的，二是（票据）可转让的，三是（道路）走得通的；
名词 negotiation 常用复数：be in negotiations、under negotiation；谈判的人叫 negotiator。并入本条。
① The union is negotiating with the management for a shorter working week.
= 工会正与资方谈判，争取缩短每周工时。
negotiate with somebody for something = 为某事与某人谈判
② The two sides have agreed to negotiate over the future of the port.
= 双方同意就港口的前途举行谈判。
negotiate over something = 就某事谈判
③ She negotiated a pay rise of eight per cent for the whole department.
= 她为整个部门谈下了百分之八的加薪。
negotiate a pay rise = 谈成加薪
negotiate a deal = 谈成一笔交易
④ The lorry could not negotiate the sharp bend and had to reverse.
= 那辆卡车拐不过那个急弯，只好倒车。
negotiate a bend = 拐过弯道
这一支指小心地通过难走的地方。
⑤ The price is negotiable if you are willing to pay in cash.
= 你要是愿意付现金，价钱可以商量。
be negotiable = 可以商量
⑥ The track is only negotiable by tractor after heavy rain.
= 大雨过后，那条土路只有拖拉机走得通。
negotiable by something = 只有某种车走得通
⑦ The contract is still under negotiation and may not be signed this month.
= 合同还在谈，这个月不一定签得下来。
under negotiation = 在谈判中
⑧ Negotiations between the airline and its pilots broke down on Friday.
= 航空公司与飞行员之间的谈判星期五破裂了。
negotiations break down = 谈判破裂
be in negotiations with somebody = 正与某人谈判
⑨ She is a tough negotiator who has never lost her temper at the table.
= 她是个强硬的谈判者，在谈判桌上从没发过脾气。
a tough negotiator = 强硬的谈判者

negritude
/ˈneɡrɪtjuːd/
核心：两支，不可数，文化史用语。一是黑人性、黑人文化认同：对非洲文化传统的肯定与自豪；
二是二十世纪三十年代由法语区黑人作家发起的那场文学与思想运动，常大写。
① The poem celebrates negritude at a time when African culture was dismissed in the schools.
= 那首诗颂扬黑人文化认同，而当时学校里是瞧不起非洲文化的。
celebrate negritude = 颂扬黑人文化认同
② He wrote his thesis on the poets of the Negritude movement in Paris.
= 他的论文写的是巴黎「黑人性」运动的诗人。
the Negritude movement = 「黑人性」运动

Negro
/ˈniːɡrəʊ/
核心：（旧，如今视为冒犯）黑人。二十世纪中叶以前是通行的中性说法，如今只见于历史文献、引文和若干固定名称，
自己说话写文章不要用，改说 black person，在美国也说 African American。复数 Negroes。
指女性的 Negress 更旧，冒犯意味更重。Negro spiritual 是黑人灵歌，如今多只说 spiritual。并入本条。
① The Negro Leagues gave black baseball players a professional stage until the sport was integrated.
= 在棒球取消种族隔离之前，黑人联盟给了黑人球员一个职业舞台。
the Negro Leagues = 黑人棒球联盟（历史名称）
Negro = 黑人（旧称，现视为冒犯）
② The painting long catalogued as Portrait of a Negress was given a different title by the museum in 2019.
= 那幅画在目录里长期题作《一位黑人女子的肖像》，二〇一九年博物馆给它换了标题。
Negress = 黑人女性（旧称，冒犯）
③ The choir ended the concert with a Negro spiritual first written down in the 1860s.
= 合唱团以一首十九世纪六十年代最早记谱的黑人灵歌结束了音乐会。
a Negro spiritual = 黑人灵歌

neigh
/neɪ/
核心：两支。一是动词，（马）嘶鸣；二是名词，马的嘶鸣声，可数。读音同 nay。
① The horses neighed and stamped when they smelled smoke from the barn.
= 马闻到谷仓飘来的烟味，又嘶叫又跺蹄。
a horse neighs = 马嘶鸣
② A loud neigh from the stable told her that the pony had heard the car.
= 马厩里一声响亮的嘶鸣，让她知道小马听见了汽车声。
a loud neigh = 一声响亮的嘶鸣

neighbour
/ˈneɪbə(r)/
核心：名词三支。一是邻居：住在隔壁或附近的人；next-door neighbour 是隔壁邻居；
二是相邻的人、物、国家：坐在旁边的人、邻国；三是（书面）同胞、身边的人：love your neighbour。
动词是（正式）与…相邻。形容词 neighbouring（邻近的，只作定语）并入本条。美式拼作 neighbor。
① Our next-door neighbours feed the cat whenever we go away.
= 我们一出门，隔壁邻居就帮忙喂猫。
a next-door neighbour = 隔壁邻居
neighbour = 邻居
② She has been a good neighbour to the old couple across the road.
= 对马路对面那对老夫妇，她一直是个好邻居。
a good neighbour = 好邻居
③ He turned to his neighbour at dinner and asked what the speech had been about.
= 吃饭时他转向邻座，问那篇讲话讲的是什么。
your neighbour at dinner = 饭桌上的邻座
④ The country has signed trade agreements with all four of its neighbours.
= 该国已同四个邻国全部签了贸易协定。
a country's neighbours = 邻国
⑤ The priest reminded them of the duty to love your neighbour.
= 神父提醒他们，有爱人如己的本分。
love your neighbour = 爱人如己
⑥ The farm neighbours a military airfield, and the cows no longer look up.
= 那座农场紧挨着一个军用机场，奶牛早就不抬头看了。
neighbour something = 与某物相邻
⑦ Children come to the school by bus from the neighbouring villages.
= 孩子们从邻近的村子坐公共汽车来上学。
neighbouring villages = 邻近的村子
neighbouring = 邻近的

neighbourhood
/ˈneɪbəhʊd/
核心：三支。一是街区、居民区：城镇里的一片地方，也指住在那儿的人，可数；二是附近、周围一带，单数：
in the neighbourhood；三是 in the neighbourhood of，（数目）大约。
neighbourhood watch 是居民自己组织的邻里守望，防盗用，并入本条。美式拼作 neighborhood。
① They grew up in a poor neighbourhood on the east side of the city.
= 他们在城东一个贫穷的街区长大。
a poor neighbourhood = 贫穷的街区
neighbourhood = 街区
② The whole neighbourhood turned out to look for the missing child.
= 整个街区的人都出来找那个走失的孩子。
the whole neighbourhood = 整个街区的人
③ Is there a decent bakery anywhere in the neighbourhood?
= 这附近有没有像样的面包店？
in the neighbourhood = 在附近
④ The repairs will cost in the neighbourhood of five thousand pounds.
= 修理费大约要五千英镑。
in the neighbourhood of = 大约
⑤ Burglaries fell by a third after the street set up a neighbourhood watch.
= 那条街成立邻里守望之后，入室盗窃少了三分之一。
a neighbourhood watch = 邻里守望

neighbourly
/ˈneɪbəli/
核心：形容词，像好邻居那样的：友善、肯帮忙。多说举动、关系、气氛。名词 neighbourliness 并入本条。
美式拼作 neighborly。
① It was neighbourly of her to take in the parcels while we were away.
= 我们不在的时候她帮忙代收包裹，真是够邻居的。
it is neighbourly of somebody to do something = 某人做某事很够邻居
② The two families have stayed on neighbourly terms despite the argument over the fence.
= 尽管为篱笆吵过，两家人还是保持着邻里和气。
on neighbourly terms = 邻里和睦
③ The street is known for its neighbourliness, and nobody locks the back door.
= 那条街以邻里和睦出名，谁家都不锁后门。
neighbourliness = 邻里和睦

neither
/ˈnaɪðə(r)/（常见）/ˈniːðə(r)/（也读）
核心：只管两个人或两样东西，三个以上用 none。四种用法。
一是限定词，接单数名词：neither answer is right。二是代词：neither of 加复数名词或代词，正式文体动词用单数，口语里也用复数。
三是 neither … nor …，既不…也不…：动词跟靠近它的那个主语一致。
四是副词，放在句首表示「也不」，后面倒装：Neither do I；口语里说 Me neither。
① Neither answer is correct, so try the sum again.
= 两个答案都不对，再算一遍吧。
neither + 单数名词 = 两个都不
neither = 两者都不
② Neither of my parents drives, so we went everywhere by bus.
= 我父母都不开车，所以我们去哪儿都坐公共汽车。
neither of somebody = 两个人都不
③ "Tea or coffee?" "Neither, thank you. A glass of water will do."
= 「茶还是咖啡？」「都不要，谢谢。来杯水就行。」
Neither, thank you. = 都不要，谢谢。
④ The hotel is neither cheap nor clean, and the lift is broken.
= 那家旅馆既不便宜也不干净，电梯还是坏的。
neither … nor … = 既不…也不…
⑤ Neither the manager nor his assistants were told about the inspection.
= 经理和他的几个助手，事先都没人告诉他们要检查。
neither A nor B = 甲和乙都不
动词跟靠近它的 assistants 一致，所以用复数。
⑥ "I cannot swim." "Neither can I, so let us stay in the boat."
= 「我不会游泳。」「我也不会，那咱们就待在船上吧。」
Neither can I. = 我也不会。
句首表示「也不」，后面主语和助动词要倒装。
⑦ "I do not like the look of that sky." "Me neither."
= 「我看这天色不妙。」「我也觉得。」
Me neither. = 我也不。
口语说法。

nelson
/ˈnelsən/
核心：两支，摔跤用语，可数。一是从背后把胳膊穿过对方腋下、压住后颈的擒拿手法；两只胳膊都用上叫 a full nelson，
只用一只叫 a half nelson；二是泛指被人这样牢牢制住的处境。
① The wrestler caught his opponent in a half nelson and forced him to the mat.
= 那名摔跤手用单臂扼颈制住对手，把他按倒在垫子上。
a half nelson = 单臂扼颈
② The doorman held the drunk in a full nelson until the police arrived.
= 门卫用双臂扼颈把那个醉汉制住，一直到警察赶来。
a full nelson = 双臂扼颈

nematode
/ˈnemətəʊd/
核心：线虫：身体细长、不分节的一大类虫，有的生活在土壤里，有的寄生在动植物体内。可数，也说 nematode worm。
① Gardeners water nematodes into the soil to kill slugs without poison.
= 园丁把线虫随水浇进土里杀鼻涕虫，不用毒药。
nematodes = 线虫
② The potato crop was ruined by a nematode that attacks the roots.
= 一种专攻根部的线虫毁了那一季的土豆。
a nematode = 一种线虫

nemesis
/ˈneməsɪs/
核心：两支，正式。一是克星、宿敌：一再把某人打败的对手，可数，复数 nemeses；
二是报应、天谴：逃不掉的应得惩罚，不可数。出自希腊神话的复仇女神。
① In the final she met her old nemesis, who had beaten her three years running.
= 决赛里她碰上了老克星，那人连着三年赢了她。
somebody's nemesis = 某人的克星
② The exam in mathematics proved to be his nemesis once again.
= 数学考试又一次成了他过不去的坎。
prove to be somebody's nemesis = 成了某人过不去的坎
③ The dictator's fall was seen by many as nemesis for years of cruelty.
= 那个独裁者的垮台，在许多人看来是多年暴行的报应。
nemesis for something = 对某事的报应

neoclassical
/ˌniːəʊˈklæsɪkəl/
核心：新古典主义的：十八、十九世纪仿效古希腊罗马风格的建筑、美术、音乐；经济学里指新古典学派的。
多作定语。名词 neoclassicism 并入本条。
① The bank is a neoclassical building with six white columns across the front.
= 那家银行是一座新古典主义建筑，正面有六根白色立柱。
a neoclassical building = 新古典主义建筑
② Neoclassical economics assumes that buyers and sellers act rationally.
= 新古典经济学假定买卖双方的行为是理性的。
neoclassical economics = 新古典经济学
③ The exhibition traces the rise of neoclassicism after the ruins of Pompeii were dug up.
= 那场展览追溯了庞贝遗址发掘之后新古典主义的兴起。
neoclassicism = 新古典主义

neocolonialism
/ˌniːəʊkəˈləʊniəlɪzəm/
核心：新殖民主义：强国不靠直接统治，而靠经济、政治、文化手段控制别国的做法。不可数，含贬义。
① Critics describe the loans as a form of neocolonialism that keeps poor countries in debt.
= 批评者把那些贷款说成一种新殖民主义，让穷国永远背着债。
a form of neocolonialism = 一种新殖民主义
② The president accused foreign mining firms of neocolonialism in his speech to parliament.
= 总统在议会讲话时，指责外国矿业公司搞新殖民主义。
accuse somebody of neocolonialism = 指责某人搞新殖民主义

neoconservative
/ˌniːəʊkənˈsɜːvətɪv/
核心：两支。一是形容词，新保守主义的：主张自由市场、对外强硬并用武力推行本国价值，多说美国政治；
二是名词，新保守主义者，可数，口语截短成 neocon。
① The paper has taken a neoconservative line on foreign policy for twenty years.
= 那家报纸在外交政策上持新保守主义立场已有二十年。
a neoconservative line = 新保守主义立场
② The neoconservatives in the cabinet argued for sending troops at once.
= 内阁里的新保守主义者主张立刻出兵。
a neoconservative = 新保守主义者
a neocon = 新保守派（口语）

neocortex
/ˌniːəʊˈkɔːteks/
核心：新皮层：哺乳动物大脑皮层里进化上最晚出现的部分，管感知、语言、推理这些高级功能。可数，单数居多，解剖学用语。
复数 neocortices。
① The human neocortex is folded so that more of it fits inside the skull.
= 人的新皮层是折叠起来的，这样颅腔里才装得下更多。
the neocortex = 新皮层
② Damage to that part of the neocortex left him unable to recognise faces.
= 新皮层那一部分受损，让他认不出人脸了。
damage to the neocortex = 新皮层受损

neodymium
/ˌniːəʊˈdɪmiəm/
核心：钕：一种金属元素，属稀土，化学符号 Nd。不可数。日常多见于 neodymium magnet，即钕磁铁，一种体积小、磁力极强的磁铁。
① Neodymium is mined mainly in China and is essential to electric motors.
= 钕主要在中国开采，是电动机不可或缺的材料。
neodymium = 钕
② A neodymium magnet the size of a coin can lift a bag of sugar.
= 一块硬币大小的钕磁铁，吸得起一袋糖。
a neodymium magnet = 钕磁铁

neoliberal
/ˌniːəʊˈlɪbərəl/
核心：两支。一是形容词，新自由主义的：主张自由市场、减少政府干预、把公营事业私有化；二是名词，新自由主义者，可数。
多用于批评的语境。名词 neoliberalism 并入本条。
① The party abandoned its neoliberal policies after losing two elections in a row.
= 连输两次大选之后，那个党放弃了新自由主义政策。
neoliberal policies = 新自由主义政策
② The neoliberals wanted to sell the railways and cut income tax.
= 新自由主义者想把铁路卖掉，并削减所得税。
a neoliberal = 新自由主义者
③ The book blames neoliberalism for the widening gap between rich and poor.
= 那本书把贫富差距拉大归咎于新自由主义。
neoliberalism = 新自由主义

Neolithic
/ˌniːəˈlɪθɪk/
核心：新石器时代的：石器时代的最后一段，人类开始务农、定居、制陶。形容词，多作定语；the Neolithic 作名词指这个时代。
首字母大写。
① The farmer's plough turned up a Neolithic axe head made of polished stone.
= 那个农夫的犁翻出了一把磨光石头做的新石器时代斧头。
a Neolithic axe = 新石器时代的斧子
② The stone circle was built by Neolithic farmers about five thousand years ago.
= 那个石圈是新石器时代的农人在大约五千年前建的。
Neolithic farmers = 新石器时代的农人
③ Pottery first appears in this region at the beginning of the Neolithic.
= 在这个地区，陶器最早出现在新石器时代之初。
the Neolithic = 新石器时代

neologism
/niˈɒlədʒɪzəm/
核心：新词、新造的词语，也指旧词的新用法。可数，语言学与评论用语。
① The dictionary adds several hundred neologisms from the internet every year.
= 那部词典每年增收几百个来自互联网的新词。
a neologism = 新词
② The essay is so full of neologisms that it has to be read with a glossary.
= 那篇文章里新造的词太多，得对着词汇表才读得下去。
full of neologisms = 满是新造的词

neon
/ˈniːɒn/
核心：两支。一是氖：一种惰性气体，通电后发出橙红色的光，化学符号 Ne，不可数；
二是霓虹灯、霓虹灯光，不可数，常作定语：a neon sign、neon lights；也说颜色鲜亮得刺眼的：neon pink。
① Neon glows orange-red when an electric current passes through it.
= 氖通上电流，会发出橙红色的光。
neon = 氖
② A neon sign above the door flashed the name of the bar in blue.
= 门上方的霓虹招牌用蓝光一闪一闪地打出酒吧的名字。
a neon sign = 霓虹招牌
③ The neon lights of the main street were reflected in the wet pavement.
= 主街的霓虹灯光倒映在湿漉漉的人行道上。
neon lights = 霓虹灯
④ The runners wore neon yellow vests so that drivers could see them at dusk.
= 跑步的人穿着荧光黄的背心，好让司机在黄昏时看得见他们。
neon yellow = 荧光黄

neonatal
/ˌniːəʊˈneɪtəl/
核心：新生儿的：指出生后头四个星期的婴儿，医学用语，只作定语。
名词 neonate 是新生儿，术语，日常说 newborn baby 的地方医学上用它。并入本条。
① The twins spent their first three weeks in the neonatal unit.
= 那对双胞胎头三个星期是在新生儿病房度过的。
a neonatal unit = 新生儿病房
② Better neonatal care has halved the death rate among premature babies.
= 新生儿护理的改善，使早产儿的死亡率减了一半。
neonatal care = 新生儿护理
③ The drug is safe for adults but has not been tested on neonates.
= 那种药对成人是安全的，但没有在新生儿身上试验过。
a neonate = 新生儿

neophyte
/ˈniːəfaɪt/
核心：两支，正式，可数。一是新手、初学者，介词用 at、in；二是（宗教）新入教的人、新进修道院的人。
① The course is designed for complete neophytes who have never held a camera.
= 那门课是为从没拿过相机的纯新手开的。
a complete neophyte = 纯新手
② As a political neophyte, she was surprised by how little the debates changed.
= 她在政界是个新手，没想到辩论能改变的东西那么少。
a political neophyte = 政界新手
③ The neophytes rise at four and spend the first hour in silent prayer.
= 新入会的修士四点起床，头一个钟头默祷。
the neophytes = 新入会的修士

neoplastic
/ˌniːəʊˈplæstɪk/
核心：两支，医学用语，只作定语。一是肿瘤的、赘生物的；二是（细胞、组织）发生了肿瘤性改变的。
① The biopsy found neoplastic cells in two of the five samples.
= 活检在五份样本中的两份里发现了肿瘤细胞。
neoplastic cells = 肿瘤细胞
② Neoplastic disease is the second most common cause of death in older dogs.
= 肿瘤性疾病是老年犬的第二大死因。
neoplastic disease = 肿瘤性疾病

neoprene
/ˈniːəpriːn/
核心：两支，不可数。一是氯丁橡胶：一种耐油、防水的合成橡胶；二是作定语，用它做的，最常见的是潜水服和电脑保护套。
① Wetsuits are made of neoprene, which traps a thin layer of water against the skin.
= 潜水服是氯丁橡胶做的，它能在皮肤外面留住薄薄一层水。
made of neoprene = 氯丁橡胶做的
② She carries her laptop in a neoprene sleeve inside her rucksack.
= 她把笔记本电脑装在氯丁橡胶套里，再放进背包。
a neoprene sleeve = 氯丁橡胶保护套

Nepalese
/ˌnepəˈliːz/
核心：两支。一是形容词，尼泊尔的；二是名词，尼泊尔人，单复数同形。首字母大写。也说 Nepali，后者还指尼泊尔语。
① The Nepalese guides carried twice as much as the climbers who had hired them.
= 尼泊尔向导背的东西，是雇他们的登山者的两倍。
Nepalese guides = 尼泊尔向导
② Several thousand Nepalese work on building sites in the Gulf.
= 有几千名尼泊尔人在海湾地区的建筑工地干活。
the Nepalese = 尼泊尔人

nephew
/ˈnefjuː/
核心：侄子、外甥：兄弟姐妹的儿子，也包括配偶的兄弟姐妹的儿子。可数。英语不分父系母系，对应的女性是 niece。
① Her nephew is staying with her while his parents are abroad.
= 她侄子在父母出国期间住在她家。
nephew = 侄子、外甥
somebody's nephew = 某人的侄子
② He left the farm to a nephew he had met only twice.
= 他把农场留给了一个只见过两面的外甥。
leave something to a nephew = 把某物留给外甥
③ She has four nieces and nephews and remembers every one of their birthdays.
= 她有四个侄子侄女，每个人的生日都记得。
nieces and nephews = 侄子侄女

nepotism
/ˈnepətɪzəm/
核心：任人唯亲、裙带关系：掌权的人把职位、好处给自己的亲戚朋友。不可数，含贬义。
① He was accused of nepotism after making his son-in-law finance director.
= 他让女婿当了财务总监，之后被指任人唯亲。
be accused of nepotism = 被指任人唯亲
② Nepotism is so common in the industry that nobody bothers to hide it.
= 裙带关系在这一行太普遍，没人费心去遮掩。
nepotism = 裙带关系

Neptune
/ˈneptjuːn/
核心：两支，首字母大写，不加冠词。一是海王星：太阳系里离太阳最远的行星；二是（罗马神话）海神尼普顿，手持三叉戟。
① Neptune takes a hundred and sixty-five years to travel once round the sun.
= 海王星绕太阳一周要一百六十五年。
Neptune = 海王星
② A bronze Neptune stands in the fountain with a trident in his hand.
= 喷泉里立着一尊青铜海神像，手持三叉戟。
Neptune = 海神尼普顿

neptunium
/nepˈtjuːniəm/
核心：镎：一种放射性金属元素，化学符号 Np，是核反应堆的副产物。不可数。
① Neptunium was the first element heavier than uranium to be made in a laboratory.
= 镎是实验室里造出的头一种比铀重的元素。
neptunium = 镎
② Small amounts of neptunium are found in the waste from nuclear reactors.
= 核反应堆的废料里含有少量的镎。
amounts of neptunium = 若干量的镎

nerd
/nɜːd/
核心：两支，口语，可数。一是书呆子：只知道埋头读书、不善交际的人，含贬义；
二是对某个专门领域痴迷又在行的人，前面加定语：a computer nerd，如今常带自豪。
形容词 nerdy 并入本条。
① At school he was a nerd who spent every break in the library.
= 上学的时候他是个书呆子，每个课间都泡在图书馆。
a nerd = 书呆子
② She is a proud maths nerd who does puzzles on the bus.
= 她是个以此为荣的数学迷，在公共汽车上都做题。
a maths nerd = 数学迷
a computer nerd = 电脑迷
③ He wore nerdy glasses long before they became fashionable.
= 早在那种书呆子眼镜流行起来之前，他就戴着了。
nerdy glasses = 书呆子眼镜
nerdy = 书呆子气的

nerve
/nɜːv/
核心：名词四支。一是神经：把感觉和指令在大脑与身体之间传递的纤维，可数；
二是 nerves，复数，紧张不安的情绪，也指承受压力的心理素质：calm your nerves；
三是勇气、胆量，不可数：it takes nerve to do something；四是（口语）厚脸皮、放肆，单数：have a nerve、what a nerve。
动词用于 nerve yourself to do something，是（正式）鼓起勇气去做。
习语：get on somebody's nerves 是惹人心烦；lose your nerve 是临阵胆怯；hold your nerve 是沉住气；
a bundle of nerves 是紧张得不得了；have the nerve to 是有胆量、居然好意思；touch a nerve 是戳到痛处；
nerves of steel 是钢铁般的意志。
形容词 nerveless 一是（手指）没力气、麻木的，二是镇定无畏的。
nerve agent 与 nerve gas 是神经毒剂、神经毒气；nerve centre 是神经中枢，引申为指挥中心；
nerve-racking 是让人神经紧绷的，也拼作 nerve-wracking。并入本条。
① The dentist said the pain came from a damaged nerve in the tooth.
= 牙医说，疼是因为牙里的一根神经受了损伤。
nerve = 神经
a damaged nerve = 受损的神经
② She took a few deep breaths to calm her nerves before going on stage.
= 上台之前，她深吸了几口气稳定情绪。
calm your nerves = 稳定情绪
③ He suffers from nerves before every match and cannot eat breakfast.
= 他每场比赛之前都紧张，吃不下早饭。
suffer from nerves = 紧张不安
④ It takes nerve to stand up and disagree with the whole room.
= 站起来跟满屋子的人唱反调，是要胆量的。
it takes nerve to do something = 做某事要胆量
⑤ She had the nerve to ask for a refund after wearing the dress to a party.
= 她穿着那条裙子去过聚会之后，居然好意思要求退款。
have the nerve to = 有胆量、厚脸皮
have the nerve to do something = 居然好意思做某事
⑥ He turned up two hours late and then complained about the food. What a nerve!
= 他晚到两个钟头，还抱怨饭菜。脸皮真厚！
What a nerve! = 脸皮真厚！
⑦ That dripping tap is beginning to get on my nerves.
= 那个滴水的龙头开始让我心烦了。
get on somebody's nerves = 惹某人心烦
⑧ He climbed to the top board, lost his nerve and came down the ladder again.
= 他爬上最高的跳板，胆怯了，又顺着梯子下来了。
lose your nerve = 临阵胆怯
⑨ The goalkeeper held her nerve and saved the last penalty.
= 守门员沉住了气，扑出了最后一个点球。
hold your nerve = 沉住气
⑩ By the morning of the interview he was a bundle of nerves.
= 到面试那天早上，他已经紧张得不得了。
a bundle of nerves = 紧张得不得了
⑪ The question about his father clearly touched a nerve.
= 那个关于他父亲的问题，显然戳到了痛处。
touch a nerve = 戳到痛处
⑫ You would have to have nerves of steel to land a plane in that wind.
= 在那样的风里降落飞机，非得有钢铁般的意志不可。
nerves of steel = 钢铁般的意志
⑬ She nerved herself to open the envelope from the hospital.
= 她鼓起勇气，拆开了医院寄来的那封信。
nerve yourself to do something = 鼓起勇气做某事
⑭ The pen dropped from his nerveless fingers and rolled under the bed.
= 钢笔从他无力的手指间掉下去，滚到了床底下。
nerveless fingers = 无力的手指
⑮ The inspectors found traces of a nerve agent on the door handle.
= 检查人员在门把手上发现了神经毒剂的痕迹。
a nerve agent = 神经毒剂
nerve gas = 神经毒气
⑯ The control room is the nerve centre of the whole railway.
= 控制室是整条铁路的神经中枢。
the nerve centre = 神经中枢、指挥中心
⑰ Waiting for the result was the most nerve-racking hour of her life.
= 等结果的那一个钟头，是她一生中最提心吊胆的时刻。
nerve-racking = 让人神经紧绷的

nervous
/ˈnɜːvəs/
核心：三支。一是紧张的、担忧的：介词用 about、of；a nervous wreck 是紧张到崩溃的人；
二是容易紧张的、神经质的：a nervous disposition；三是神经系统的，只作定语。
跟 anxious 的分别：这个词偏指事前的发慌，anxious 偏指为结果悬着心。
副词 nervously 与名词 nervousness 并入本条；
nervous system 是神经系统，nervous breakdown 是精神崩溃，也并入。
① I always get nervous before a flight, however short it is.
= 不管航程多短，坐飞机之前我总是紧张。
nervous = 紧张的
get nervous = 紧张起来
② She was nervous about meeting his parents for the first time.
= 头一回见他父母，她心里发慌。
be nervous about something = 为某事紧张
③ The horse is nervous of traffic and should not be ridden on the road.
= 那匹马怕车，不该骑上公路。
be nervous of something = 害怕某物
④ He was a thin, nervous man who jumped whenever the telephone rang.
= 他是个瘦削、神经质的人，电话一响就吓一跳。
a nervous man = 神经质的人
⑤ By the end of the trial she was a nervous wreck.
= 到庭审结束的时候，她已经紧张得快垮了。
a nervous wreck = 紧张到崩溃的人
⑥ The disease attacks the nervous system and slowly weakens the muscles.
= 那种病侵袭神经系统，让肌肉慢慢衰弱。
the nervous system = 神经系统
⑦ He had a nervous breakdown after working eighty hours a week for a year.
= 他一连一年每周干八十个小时，之后精神崩溃了。
a nervous breakdown = 精神崩溃
⑧ She laughed nervously and looked at the door.
= 她不安地笑了笑，朝门口看了一眼。
laugh nervously = 不安地笑
⑨ He tried to hide his nervousness by talking too much.
= 他话说个不停，想掩饰自己的紧张。
hide your nervousness = 掩饰紧张

nervy
/ˈnɜːvi/
核心：两支，口语，英美意思相反。一是（英式）紧张不安的、心神不定的；二是（美式）胆大的、放肆的。
① She has been nervy and short-tempered since the burglary.
= 自从家里遭了贼，她一直心神不定，动不动就发火。
be nervy = 心神不定
② It was a nervy move to ask the chairman for his own office.
= 开口跟董事长要他本人的办公室，这一招真够放肆的。
a nervy move = 放肆的一招
这一支是美式用法。

ness
/nes/
核心：两支，可数。一是岬、海角：伸进海里的一块陆地；二是这个词留在英国地名里的用法，多在东海岸与苏格兰。
① The lighthouse stands at the end of a shingle ness that grows a little every year.
= 那座灯塔立在一道卵石岬角的尽头，那岬角每年都长出去一点。
a ness = 岬角
② The path follows the cliff as far as the ness and then turns inland.
= 那条小路沿着悬崖一直到海角，然后拐向内陆。
as far as the ness = 一直到海角

nest
/nest/
核心：名词三支。一是鸟巢、窝：鸟筑来下蛋育雏的，也指昆虫、小动物的巢穴：a wasps' nest；
二是（比喻）安乐窝、家：leave the nest 与 fly the nest 是（孩子）长大离家；三是套在一起的一组东西：a nest of tables。
动词是筑巢；计算机上指嵌套。习语 feather your nest 是中饱私囊；empty nest 是孩子都离家后的空巢。
nest box 是人挂在树上给鸟住的巢箱；nest egg 是攒着备用的一笔积蓄。并入本条。
① A pair of swallows built a nest under the roof of the garage.
= 一对燕子在车库的屋檐下筑了巢。
nest = 巢
build a nest = 筑巢
② He was stung six times when he disturbed a wasps' nest in the shed.
= 他惊动了棚子里的一个黄蜂窝，被蜇了六下。
a wasps' nest = 黄蜂窝
③ Their youngest child leaves the nest in September to go to college.
= 他们最小的孩子九月离家去上大学。
leave the nest = 离巢、独立
④ All three children had flown the nest by the time she was fifty.
= 她五十岁的时候，三个孩子都已经离家了。
fly the nest = 长大离家
⑤ The house felt enormous once it had become an empty nest.
= 孩子们一走成了空巢，那座房子显得大极了。
an empty nest = 空巢
⑥ A nest of three small tables stood beside the sofa.
= 沙发旁边摆着一套三张叠放的小桌子。
a nest of tables = 一套叠放的桌子
⑦ Gulls nest on the ledges of the cliff every spring.
= 每年春天，海鸥在悬崖的岩架上筑巢。
nest on something = 在某处筑巢
⑧ The minister was accused of feathering his own nest with public money.
= 那位大臣被指用公款中饱私囊。
feather your nest = 中饱私囊
⑨ We put up a nest box on the apple tree, and blue tits moved in within a week.
= 我们在苹果树上挂了个巢箱，不到一个星期蓝山雀就搬进去了。
a nest box = 巢箱
⑩ She has built up a small nest egg for her retirement.
= 她攒了一小笔积蓄留着养老。
nest egg = 备用的积蓄
a nest egg = 一笔积蓄

nestle
/ˈnesəl/
核心：两支，字母 t 不发音。一是舒服地依偎、蜷着：介词用 against、into、among，也可及物：nestle your head；
二是（房屋、村庄）坐落在有遮蔽的地方。
① The child nestled against her mother and fell asleep before the film ended.
= 孩子依偎在妈妈身上，电影没放完就睡着了。
nestle against somebody = 依偎着某人
② She nestled her head on his shoulder as the train left the station.
= 火车驶出车站的时候，她把头靠在他肩上。
nestle your head on something = 把头靠在某处
③ The village nestles in a valley between two wooded hills.
= 那座村庄坐落在两座林木茂密的小山之间的谷地里。
nestle in a valley = 坐落在山谷里

nestling
/ˈnestlɪŋ/
核心：两支，可数。一是雏鸟：还没长好羽毛、离不了巢的小鸟；二是（文学）拿来比喻受呵护的幼小者。
① The nestlings opened their beaks wide whenever a shadow fell across them.
= 只要有影子落在身上，那些雏鸟就把嘴张得大大的。
a nestling = 雏鸟
② A nestling that falls to the ground should be put back if the parents are nearby.
= 雏鸟掉到地上，要是亲鸟就在附近，应当把它放回去。
a nestling falls = 雏鸟掉落

net
/net/
核心：两个词。其一是名词「网」：一是网状的织物，不可数；二是有具体用途的一张网，可数：a fishing net、a mosquito net；
三是球类运动里的球网、球门网；四是 the Net，互联网，旧一些的说法。
动词是用网捕；也指把球打进网，以及（口语）弄到手。
习语 slip through the net 是成了漏网之鱼、没被照顾到；cast your net wide 是广撒网；a safety net 是安全网、保障。
其二是形容词「净的」：扣除税、费用、包装之后剩下的，跟 gross 相对：net profit、net weight；
the net result 是最终结果。动词是净赚。英式也拼作 nett。
net curtain 是纱帘；net neutrality 是网络中立；net surfer 是（旧）上网冲浪的人。并入本条。
① The curtains are made of fine net that lets in the light.
= 那些帘子是用细网纱做的，透光。
net = 网
fine net = 细网纱
② The fishermen spread their nets on the harbour wall to dry.
= 渔民把网摊在港口的堤墙上晾干。
a fishing net = 渔网
③ We slept under a mosquito net with the windows wide open.
= 我们开着窗，睡在蚊帐里。
a mosquito net = 蚊帐
④ Her second serve hit the top of the net and dropped back on her own side.
= 她的二发球打在网带上，落回了自己这一边。
the net = 球网
⑤ He put the ball in the back of the net in the last minute.
= 最后一分钟，他把球送进了网窝。
the back of the net = 网窝
⑥ They netted enough fish in one morning to feed the village for a week.
= 他们一个上午网到的鱼，够全村吃一个星期。
net fish = 用网捕鱼
⑦ The company's net profit rose to four million pounds last year.
= 那家公司去年的净利润增至四百万英镑。
net = 净的
net profit = 净利润
⑧ The net weight of the tin is four hundred grams.
= 那听罐头的净重是四百克。
net weight = 净重
⑨ The net result of all the changes is that we pay more for less.
= 这一连串变动的最终结果是，我们花的钱多了，得到的少了。
the net result = 最终结果
⑩ The sale of the house netted them a quarter of a million.
= 卖那座房子让他们净赚二十五万。
net somebody something = 让某人净赚
⑪ She earns sixty thousand gross, which is about forty-two thousand nett.
= 她税前挣六万，税后大约四万二。
nett = 净的（net 的英式拼法）
⑫ Too many children with reading problems slip through the net.
= 太多有阅读障碍的孩子成了没人管的漏网之鱼。
slip through the net = 成了漏网之鱼
⑬ The firm cast its net wide and interviewed candidates from six countries.
= 那家公司广撒网，面试了来自六个国家的应聘者。
cast your net wide = 广撒网
⑭ The benefit acts as a safety net for people who lose their jobs.
= 那项补助对失业的人起着安全网的作用。
a safety net = 安全网、保障
⑮ She twitched the net curtain aside to see who was at the gate.
= 她把纱帘拨开一点，看大门口是谁。
a net curtain = 纱帘
⑯ Supporters of net neutrality say that providers should treat all traffic alike.
= 支持网络中立的人说，运营商应当对所有流量一视同仁。
net neutrality = 网络中立
⑰ In those days a net surfer waited a full minute for each page to load.
= 那个年代，上网冲浪的人每等一页加载出来要足足一分钟。
a net surfer = 上网冲浪的人

netball
/ˈnetbɔːl/
核心：两支。一是无板篮球、英式女子篮球：七人一队，把球投进一个没有篮板的高篮圈，不许运球，在英国和英联邦国家流行，不可数；
二是这项运动用的球，可数。
① She plays netball for the county and trains three evenings a week.
= 她代表郡队打无板篮球，每周训练三个晚上。
play netball = 打无板篮球
② The netball rolled under the bench and nobody could reach it.
= 那只球滚到长凳底下，谁也够不着。
a netball = 无板篮球用球

netbook
/ˈnetbʊk/
核心：两支，可数。一是上网本：二〇〇八年前后流行的一种小而便宜的笔记本电脑，主要用来上网；二是泛指这类轻便的小电脑。
① He wrote the whole thesis on a netbook with a ten-inch screen.
= 他整篇论文都是在一台十英寸屏的上网本上写的。
a netbook = 上网本
② Netbooks sold in their millions until tablets took over the market.
= 上网本卖了几百万台，直到平板电脑占了市场。
netbooks = 上网本

nether
/ˈneðə(r)/
核心：两支，旧式或诙谐，只作定语。一是下面的、下部的；二是用在固定说法里：
the nether regions、nether regions 是（诙谐）下身、私处，也指阴间；the nether world 是阴间。
① The play shows the hero's descent into the nether world to bring back his wife.
= 那出戏演的是主角下到阴间，要把妻子带回来。
the nether world = 阴间
② The dog sat down in the snow and looked surprised at the cold on its nether regions.
= 那条狗一屁股坐在雪里，下身一凉，露出吃惊的样子。
nether regions = 下身（诙谐）
③ The damp had rotted the nether end of every post in the fence.
= 潮气把篱笆上每根木桩的下端都泡烂了。
the nether end = 下端

netiquette
/ˈnetɪket/
核心：网络礼仪：在网上与人交流时约定俗成的规矩，比如不全用大写字母、不乱转发。不可数，由 net 和 etiquette 拼成，如今听着有点旧。
① Typing a whole message in capital letters is considered poor netiquette.
= 整条消息都用大写字母打，被看作不懂网络礼仪。
poor netiquette = 不懂网络礼仪
② The forum has a short guide to netiquette that every member is asked to read.
= 那个论坛有一份简短的网络礼仪指南，要求每个成员都读一读。
a guide to netiquette = 网络礼仪指南

netizen
/ˈnetɪzən/
核心：网民：经常上网、参与网上讨论的人。可数，由 net 和 citizen 拼成，多见于新闻报道，尤其是谈东亚各国的网上舆论时。
① Netizens were quick to point out the mistake in the minister's post.
= 网民很快就指出了那位大臣帖子里的错误。
netizens = 网民
② The video drew angry comments from thousands of netizens within an hour.
= 那段视频一个钟头之内就招来了成千上万网民的愤怒评论。
comments from netizens = 网民的评论

netminder
/ˈnetmaɪndə(r)/
核心：守门员：冰球、足球报道里对 goalkeeper 的另一种叫法，多见于体育版，北美冰球报道里尤其常用。可数。
① The young netminder stopped forty shots and kept his team in the game.
= 那名年轻的守门员挡出四十次射门，让球队没被甩开。
a netminder = 守门员
② The club has signed a Finnish netminder to replace the injured captain.
= 俱乐部签下一名芬兰守门员，顶替受伤的队长。
sign a netminder = 签下一名守门员

netting
/ˈnetɪŋ/
核心：网状材料、网子：用线、绳、铁丝编成的整片材料，不可数。前面加定语说明材质与用途：wire netting、garden netting。
① The strawberries are covered with netting to keep the birds off.
= 草莓上罩着网子，防鸟来啄。
be covered with netting = 罩着网子
② He fenced the chicken run with wire netting sunk a foot into the ground.
= 他用铁丝网把鸡场围起来，网脚埋进地里一英尺。
wire netting = 铁丝网

nettle
/ˈnetəl/
核心：名词是荨麻：一种野草，茎叶上的细毛碰到皮肤会刺痛起疹，可数，也说 stinging nettle。
动词是（口语）惹恼、激怒，多用被动，介词用 by。
习语 grasp the nettle 是（英式）硬着头皮迎难而上。
nettlerash 是荨麻疹，医学上叫 urticaria，并入本条。
① The children ran through a patch of nettles and came out crying.
= 孩子们从一片荨麻丛里跑过去，出来的时候都哭了。
a patch of nettles = 一片荨麻
② A stinging nettle brushed her ankle and left a row of white bumps.
= 一株荨麻扫过她的脚踝，留下一排白色的小包。
a stinging nettle = 荨麻
③ He was clearly nettled by the suggestion that he had copied the idea.
= 有人暗示他抄袭了那个点子，他显然被惹恼了。
be nettled by something = 被某事惹恼
④ The council must grasp the nettle and close the schools that are half empty.
= 市政会必须硬着头皮，把那些空了一半的学校关掉。
grasp the nettle = 迎难而上
⑤ The boy came out in nettlerash after eating strawberries.
= 那个男孩吃了草莓以后起了荨麻疹。
nettlerash = 荨麻疹

nettlesome
/ˈnetəlsəm/
核心：两支，主要是美式，书面。一是（问题、事情）恼人的、棘手难缠的；二是（人）爱找麻烦、惹人烦的。
① The treaty leaves the nettlesome question of fishing rights for later talks.
= 那项条约把渔权这个棘手的问题留给以后再谈。
a nettlesome question = 棘手的问题
② She had a nettlesome neighbour who complained about every visitor's car.
= 她有个烦人的邻居，谁的车来了都要抱怨。
a nettlesome neighbour = 烦人的邻居

network
/ˈnetwɜːk/
核心：名词四支。一是（道路、管线、神经的）网状系统：a rail network；二是（人、机构组成的）关系网、人脉：
a network of friends；三是互相连接的计算机组成的网络：be on the network；四是（广播、电视）联播网。
动词一是把计算机联网；二是（为了事业）结交人脉；三是（电视）联播。
名词 networking 一是建立人脉，二是计算机联网；networker 是善于结交人脉的人，也指远程联网办公的人。
network effect 指用的人越多、对每个用户越有价值的效应；network neutrality 同 net neutrality。并入本条。
① The country has a dense rail network that reaches every town.
= 该国有稠密的铁路网，通到每一座城镇。
a rail network = 铁路网
② She built up a network of contacts during ten years in publishing.
= 她在出版界干了十年，攒下了一张人脉网。
network = 人脉、网络
a network of contacts = 人脉网
③ The printer is on the network, so anyone in the office can use it.
= 那台打印机联在网络上，办公室里谁都能用。
on the network = 联在网络上
④ The series was shown on all three of the big American networks.
= 那套系列片在美国三大电视网都播过。
a television network = 电视网
⑤ All the tills in the shop are networked to a computer in the back office.
= 店里所有的收银机都跟后面办公室的一台电脑联了网。
be networked = 联了网
⑥ He goes to conferences mainly to network and rarely hears a talk.
= 他去开会主要是为了结交人脉，很少听讲。
network = 结交人脉
⑦ Networking got her the job long before it was advertised.
= 那份工作还远没登广告，她就靠人脉拿到了。
networking = 建立人脉
⑧ He is a tireless networker who remembers the name of everyone he meets.
= 他结交人脉不知疲倦，见过的每个人的名字都记得。
a networker = 善于结交人脉的人
⑨ A messaging app depends on the network effect: it is useless until your friends join.
= 聊天应用靠的是网络效应：朋友们不加入，它就毫无用处。
the network effect = 网络效应
network neutrality = 网络中立

neural
/ˈnjʊərəl/
核心：神经的、神经系统的。只作定语，科学用语：neural pathways（神经通路）、neural activity（神经活动）。
neural network 是神经网络：一是大脑里的，二是计算机上模仿它搭建的，后者如今更常见。并入本条。
① Learning a language as a child lays down neural pathways that last for life.
= 儿时学语言所铺下的神经通路，会保持一辈子。
neural pathways = 神经通路
② The scanner shows which areas of neural activity light up when we read.
= 扫描仪能显示我们阅读时哪些区域的神经活动活跃起来。
neural activity = 神经活动
③ The software uses a neural network trained on millions of photographs.
= 那款软件用的是一个拿几百万张照片训练出来的神经网络。
a neural network = 神经网络

neuralgia
/njʊəˈrældʒə/
核心：神经痛：沿着一根神经走向发作的剧烈刺痛，多在脸部和头部。不可数，医学用语。形容词 neuralgic 并入本条。
① She suffers from neuralgia that flares up whenever the wind is cold.
= 她有神经痛的毛病，风一冷就发作。
suffer from neuralgia = 患神经痛
② The neuralgic pain shot from his jaw to his ear and was gone in a second.
= 那阵神经痛从他下巴窜到耳朵，一秒钟就过去了。
neuralgic pain = 神经痛

neurasthenia
/ˌnjʊərəsˈθiːniə/
核心：神经衰弱：旧时的诊断名称，指长期疲乏、头痛、易怒、睡不好的一组症状。不可数，如今西方医学已不大用，多见于历史与文学。
① The doctor diagnosed neurasthenia and ordered three months of rest by the sea.
= 医生诊断为神经衰弱，嘱咐到海边休养三个月。
diagnose neurasthenia = 诊断为神经衰弱
② Half the characters in the novel seem to be suffering from neurasthenia.
= 那部小说里的人物，有一半像是得了神经衰弱。
suffer from neurasthenia = 患神经衰弱

neurobiology
/ˌnjʊərəʊbaɪˈɒlədʒi/
核心：神经生物学：研究神经系统的结构、发育与功能的生物学分支。不可数。形容词 neurobiological 并入本条。
① She took a degree in neurobiology and went on to study how memory forms.
= 她念了神经生物学学位，后来研究记忆是怎样形成的。
a degree in neurobiology = 神经生物学学位
② The paper looks for a neurobiological explanation of why teenagers sleep so late.
= 那篇论文想从神经生物学上解释青少年为什么睡得那么晚。
a neurobiological explanation = 神经生物学上的解释

neurolinguistics
/ˌnjʊərəʊlɪŋˈɡwɪstɪks/
核心：神经语言学：研究大脑怎样处理、习得语言的学科。不可数，动词用单数。
neurolinguistic programming 是另一回事：一套声称能靠语言与思维习惯改变行为的自我提升方法，缩写 NLP，学界并不认可。并入本条。
① Neurolinguistics tries to explain why some stroke patients lose nouns but keep verbs.
= 神经语言学试图解释，为什么有的中风病人丢了名词却保住了动词。
neurolinguistics = 神经语言学
② The sales team was sent on a weekend course in neurolinguistic programming.
= 销售团队被派去上了一个周末的神经语言程式学课程。
neurolinguistic programming = 神经语言程式学

neurology
/njʊəˈrɒlədʒi/
核心：神经病学、神经科：研究和治疗神经系统疾病的医学分支，不可数。
形容词 neurological（神经系统的、神经病学的）与名词 neurologist（神经科医生）并入本条。
① He was referred to the department of neurology after his third blackout.
= 第三次昏厥之后，他被转到了神经科。
the department of neurology = 神经科
② The tests found no sign of neurological damage after the accident.
= 检查没有发现事故后神经系统受损的迹象。
neurological damage = 神经系统损伤
③ The neurologist asked her to touch her nose with her eyes closed.
= 神经科医生让她闭着眼睛摸自己的鼻子。
a neurologist = 神经科医生

neuron
/ˈnjʊərɒn/
核心：神经元、神经细胞：神经系统里传递电信号的基本单位。可数，英式也拼作 neurone。
motor neurone disease 是运动神经元病。
① The human brain contains roughly eighty-six billion neurons.
= 人脑大约有八百六十亿个神经元。
a neuron = 神经元
② Each neuron is connected to thousands of others by fine branches.
= 每个神经元靠细细的分支跟成千上万个别的神经元相连。
each neuron = 每个神经元
③ He was diagnosed with motor neurone disease at the age of twenty-one.
= 他二十一岁被诊断出运动神经元病。
motor neurone disease = 运动神经元病

neurophysiology
/ˌnjʊərəʊfɪziˈɒlədʒi/
核心：神经生理学：研究神经系统怎样运作的学科，着眼于电信号与化学信号。不可数。
① The lab is known for its work on the neurophysiology of sleep.
= 那个实验室以研究睡眠的神经生理而闻名。
the neurophysiology of sleep = 睡眠的神经生理
② She lectures on neurophysiology to second-year medical students.
= 她给医学院二年级的学生讲神经生理学。
lecture on neurophysiology = 讲授神经生理学

neuroscience
/ˈnjʊərəʊsaɪəns/
核心：神经科学：研究大脑与神经系统的各门学科的总称。不可数；指其中一门时可数。研究者叫 neuroscientist，并入本条。
① Advances in neuroscience have changed the way we think about addiction.
= 神经科学的进展，改变了我们对成瘾的看法。
advances in neuroscience = 神经科学的进展
② A neuroscientist explained to the jury how memories can be altered by suggestion.
= 一位神经科学家向陪审团解释，暗示怎样能改变记忆。
a neuroscientist = 神经科学家

neurosis
/njʊəˈrəʊsɪs/
核心：两支。一是（医学）神经症、神经官能症：以焦虑、恐惧、强迫为特征的心理障碍，可数或不可数，复数 neuroses；
二是（口语）过度的担忧、放不下的心病。
① The clinic treats patients with anxiety, depression and other forms of neurosis.
= 那家诊所治疗焦虑、抑郁和其他类型的神经症患者。
a form of neurosis = 一种神经症
② His worry about germs had become a neurosis that ruled the household.
= 他对细菌的担忧，已经成了支配全家的一块心病。
become a neurosis = 成了心病
③ The film gently mocks the neuroses of the city's middle class.
= 那部片子温和地取笑城里中产阶级的种种神经质。
the neuroses of somebody = 某人的种种神经质

neurosurgery
/ˈnjʊərəʊsɜːdʒəri/
核心：神经外科、神经外科手术：给大脑、脊髓、神经动的手术。不可数。做这种手术的医生叫 neurosurgeon，并入本条。
① She had neurosurgery to remove a tumour pressing on the optic nerve.
= 她做了神经外科手术，切除压迫视神经的肿瘤。
have neurosurgery = 做神经外科手术
② The neurosurgeon operated for nine hours without leaving the theatre.
= 那位神经外科医生连做九个小时手术，没出过手术室。
a neurosurgeon = 神经外科医生

neurotic
/njʊəˈrɒtɪk/
核心：两支。一是（医学）神经症的、患神经症的；二是（口语）神经质的、过分紧张焦虑的，介词用 about。
名词指神经质的人、神经症患者，可数。副词 neurotically 并入本条。
① She is neurotic about locking doors and checks each one three times.
= 她对锁门这件事很神经质，每扇门都要查三遍。
be neurotic about something = 对某事神经质
② The patient showed neurotic symptoms long before the breakdown.
= 那位病人早在崩溃之前就有神经症的症状。
neurotic symptoms = 神经症症状
③ The hero of the comedy is a charming neurotic who is afraid of lifts.
= 那出喜剧的主角是个讨人喜欢的神经质，他怕电梯。
a neurotic = 神经质的人
④ He is neurotically tidy and cannot sleep if a drawer is left open.
= 他爱整洁爱到神经质，有个抽屉没关上就睡不着。
neurotically tidy = 爱整洁爱到神经质

neurotoxin
/ˈnjʊərəʊtɒksɪn/
核心：神经毒素：损害神经系统的毒物，蛇毒、某些农药、肉毒杆菌毒素都属此类。可数。
① The snake's venom contains a neurotoxin that paralyses the muscles used for breathing.
= 那种蛇的毒液含有一种神经毒素，能让呼吸肌麻痹。
a neurotoxin = 神经毒素
② Lead is a powerful neurotoxin that is especially harmful to young children.
= 铅是一种强力的神经毒素，对幼儿危害尤其大。
a powerful neurotoxin = 强力的神经毒素

neurotransmitter
/ˈnjʊərəʊtrænzmɪtə(r)/
核心：神经递质：神经细胞之间传递信号的化学物质，多巴胺、血清素都是。可数。
① Dopamine is a neurotransmitter involved in pleasure and in movement.
= 多巴胺是一种神经递质，与愉悦感和运动有关。
a neurotransmitter = 神经递质
② The drug works by raising the level of one neurotransmitter in the brain.
= 那种药靠提高大脑里某一种神经递质的水平起作用。
the level of a neurotransmitter = 神经递质的水平

neuter
/ˈnjuːtə(r)/
核心：动词一是给（动物）绝育、阉割，多用被动；二是（贬）削弱、使失去作用。
形容词是（语法）中性的：德语、拉丁语等语言里名词的三种性之一；名词指中性词。
① All the cats at the shelter are neutered before they go to a home.
= 收容所里所有的猫，送去人家之前都做了绝育。
be neutered = 做了绝育
② The committee has been neutered by rules that stop it calling witnesses.
= 那个委员会被不许传唤证人的规定架空了。
be neutered = 被架空
这一支是比喻，含贬义。
③ The German word for "girl" is neuter, which puzzles every beginner.
= 德语里「女孩」这个词是中性的，初学者个个纳闷。
be neuter = 属中性
④ Latin nouns are masculine, feminine or neuter, and the endings differ for each.
= 拉丁语名词分阳性、阴性、中性，词尾各不相同。
masculine, feminine or neuter = 阳性、阴性或中性

neutral
/ˈnjuːtrəl/
核心：形容词五支。一是中立的、不偏向任何一方的：remain neutral；二是（国家）中立的、不参战的；
三是（表情、语气）不带感情色彩的；四是（颜色）素淡的、不抢眼的：米、灰、白一类；
五是（化学）中性的，不酸不碱；（电）不带电的。
